Let's build it. I'm following the spec's §80 protocol exactly: Phase 0 (planning) → Phase 1 (CLI foundation) only, then stop for verification before Phase 2. Since I can't execute commands in this chat, you'll be my runtime: I write, you run, we iterate. forgex doctor (built in Phase 1) becomes the permanent environment verifier.

0.2 Architecture — Layer Map
text

┌─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐
│ CLI (Typer + Rich)          thin commands, no core logic │
├─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┤
│ Orchestration               workflow engine · state ·    │
│                             events · checkpoints · retry │
├─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┤
│ Agents                      architect · builder ·       │
│                             expert_coder · supervisor    │
├──────────────────────────┬──────────────────────────────┤
│ Model layer              │ Tool layer (permission-      │
│ provider · router ·      │ gated): fs · search ·        │
│ registry · capabilities  │ terminal · git · tests · dep │
│ · fallback               │ · sandbox                    │
├──────────────────────────┴──────────────────────────────┤
│ Context         indexer · retriever · dependency graph  │
├─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┤
│ Storage         SQLite (metadata only) · filesystem =   │
│                 source of truth · .forgex/ project dir  │
└─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘
Rule enforced everywhere: CLI commands parse → call services → render. No core logic in Typer functions (spec §60), so a future server/UI calls the same services.

0.3 Core Contracts (frozen for Phase 1+)
python

# Model layer (Phase 3)
class ModelProvider(ABC):
    async def generate(self, request: ModelRequest) -> ModelResponse: ...
    async def stream(self, request: ModelRequest) -> AsyncIterator[str]: ...
    async def health(self) -> ProviderHealth: ...

# Tool layer (Phase 4)
class Tool(ABC):
    name: str
    schema: dict                      # JSON-schema for the model
    required_permissions: set[Permission]
    async def execute(self, call: ToolCall) -> ToolResult: ...

# Agent layer (Phase 5+)
class Agent(ABC):
    role: AgentRole
    permissions: PermissionSet        # per-agent allow/deny matrix (§32)
    async def run(self, state: ProjectState, ctx: RunContext) -> AgentResult: ...

# Language adapters (Phase 6+, Python first)
class LanguageAdapter(Protocol):
    def detect(self, root: Path) -> ProjectDetection: ...
    def test(self, root: Path) -> TestResult: ...
    def build(self, root: Path) -> BuildResult: ...

INITIALIZING → ANALYZING → RESEARCHING → PLANNING → BUILDING
     → REVIEWING → TESTING → SUPERVISING ─┬→ COMPLETED

0.5 Security Model (the 5 boundaries)
Boundary
Mechanism
Phase
Workspace confinement	resolve_in_workspace() — rejects .., absolute escape, symlink escape	1 ✅
Per-agent permissions	allow/deny matrix per agent role	4
Command execution	restricted executor → Docker sandbox	4 / 10
Approval gate	auto / ask / safe modes	4
Secret hygiene	keys only in env vars, never in config/logs/prompts	1 ✅ (config stores api_key_env names only)

Untrusted-data rule: repository content is data, never instructions. Enforced by prompt framing in Phase 5.

0.7 Phase 0/1 Decision Log (ADR-style, §44)
MIT license, src-layout, hatchling build backend.
tomllib (stdlib) to read TOML; tomli-w to write; Pydantic v2 for schema validation with extra="forbid" (catches typos loudly).
forgex init does not auto-run git init — warns instead. Non-destructive by default.
Git is an optional doctor check until the checkpoint system lands (Phase 6), so doctor stays deterministic on machines without git.
events.jsonl JSONL format ({"ts", "type", "data"}) is frozen now; Phase 2 formalizes it into Pydantic models on the same format.

src/forgex/schemas/error.py

python

"""FORGEX error taxonomy.

All controlled failures derive from :class:`ForgexError` so the CLI can
render friendly messages and keep raw stack traces behind ``--debug``
(spec section 28). Exit codes are stable per error class.
"""

from __future__ import annotations


class ForgexError(Exception):
    """Base class for all controlled FORGEX errors."""

    exit_code: int = 1


class ConfigError(ForgexError):
    exit_code = 2


class FileSystemError(ForgexError):
    exit_code = 3


class ProjectError(ForgexError):
    exit_code = 4

src/forgex/utils/__init__.py

python

"""Shared utilities."""
src/forgex/utils/paths.py — security boundary #1; every agent filesystem operation will route through this from Phase 4 onward.

python

"""Workspace-safe path resolution.

Every filesystem operation performed by FORGEX tools must obtain its
target path through :func:`resolve_in_workspace`. This is a core security
boundary: agents must never touch paths outside the authorized workspace.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

from pathlib import Path

FORGEX_DIR_NAME = ".forgex"

__all__ = ["PathViolationError", "find_project_root", "resolve_in_workspace"]


class PathViolationError(Exception):
    """A path attempted to escape the authorized workspace."""


def resolve_in_workspace(workspace: str | Path, target: str | Path) -> Path:
    """Resolve *target* inside *workspace* and refuse any escape.

    Rules enforced:
      * Relative targets are joined to the workspace.
      * Absolute targets must already be inside the workspace.
      * ``..`` traversal that would leave the workspace is rejected.
      * Symlinks are resolved; links pointing outside the workspace are
        rejected (containment is checked on the final real path).
      * The workspace root itself is allowed (needed for listing).
    """
    workspace = Path(workspace).resolve()
    candidate = Path(target)

    resolved = candidate.resolve() if candidate.is_absolute() else (workspace / candidate).resolve()

    if resolved != workspace and workspace not in resolved.parents:
        msg = f"path escapes workspace: {target!r} -> {resolved}"
        raise PathViolationError(msg)

    return resolved


def find_project_root(start: str | Path | None = None) -> Path | None:
    """Walk upward from *start* (default: cwd) looking for a .forgex directory."""
    current = (Path(start) if start else Path.cwd()).resolve()
    for candidate in (current, *current.parents):
        if (candidate / FORGEX_DIR_NAME).is_dir():
            return candidate
    return None
src/forgex/utils/logging.py

python

"""Internal logging for FORGEX.

Diagnostics go to stderr so stdout stays clean for --json and piped
output. Human-facing rendering lives in :mod:`forgex.cli.ui`.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import logging

_LEVELS = {
    "debug": logging.DEBUG,
    "info": logging.INFO,
    "warning": logging.WARNING,
    "error": logging.ERROR,
}


def configure_logging(level: str = "warning") -> None:
    """Configure the root ``forgex`` logger with a stderr handler."""
    logger = logging.getLogger("forgex")
    handler = logging.StreamHandler()
    handler.setFormatter(
        logging.Formatter("%(asctime)s %(levelname)-7s %(name)s: %(message)s", "%H:%M:%S")
    )
    logger.handlers.clear()
    logger.addHandler(handler)
    logger.setLevel(_LEVELS.get(level.lower(), logging.WARNING))

src/forgex/config/schema.py

python

"""Pydantic models describing FORGEX configuration.

Priority (highest wins), per spec section 27:
    CLI arguments > environment (secrets) > project .forgex/config.toml
    > global ~/.forgex/config.toml > schema defaults.

Secrets NEVER live in these files — providers store only the *name* of
the environment variable holding the key (``api_key_env``).
"""

from __future__ import annotations

from typing import Literal

from pydantic import BaseModel, ConfigDict, Field


class ProviderConfig(BaseModel):
    """A provider endpoint description. Contains no secrets."""

    type: Literal["openai_compatible", "groq", "local"] = "openai_compatible"
    base_url: str | None = None
    api_key_env: str | None = None


class AgentModelBinding(BaseModel):
    provider: str = "groq"
    model: str | None = None


class ModelsConfig(BaseModel):
    architect: AgentModelBinding = Field(default_factory=AgentModelBinding)
    builder: AgentModelBinding = Field(default_factory=AgentModelBinding)
    expert_coder: AgentModelBinding = Field(default_factory=AgentModelBinding)
    supervisor: AgentModelBinding = Field(default_factory=AgentModelBinding)

src/forgex/config/loader.py

python

"""Layered configuration loading, saving, and dotted-key editing."""

from __future__ import annotations

import tomllib
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Any

import tomli_w
from pydantic import ValidationError

from forgex.config.schema import ForgexConfig
from forgex.schemas.error import ConfigError

FORGEX_DIR_NAME = ".forgex"
CONFIG_FILE_NAME = "config.toml"


def global_config_path() -> Path:
    return Path.home() / FORGEX_DIR_NAME / CONFIG_FILE_NAME


def project_config_path(project_root: Path) -> Path:
    return project_root / FORGEX_DIR_NAME / CONFIG_FILE_NAME


def _read_toml(path: Path) -> dict[str, Any]:
    if not path.is_file():
        return {}
    try:
        with path.open("rb") as fh:
            return tomllib.load(fh)
    except tomllib.TOMLDecodeError as exc:
        msg = f"Invalid TOML in {path}: {exc}"
        raise ConfigError(msg) from exc


def _deep_merge(base: dict[str, Any], override: dict[str, Any]) -> dict[str, Any]:
    merged = dict(base)
    for key, value in override.items():
        if isinstance(value, dict) and isinstance(merged.get(key), dict):
            merged[key] = _deep_merge(merged[key], value)
        else:
            merged[key] = value
    return merged


def load_config(
    project_root: Path | None = None,
    overrides: dict[str, Any] | None = None,
) -> ForgexConfig:
    """Load merged configuration: defaults < global < project < overrides."""
    data: dict[str, Any] = ForgexConfig().model_dump()
    data = _deep_merge(data, _read_toml(global_config_path()))
    if project_root is not None:
        data = _deep_merge(data, _read_toml(project_config_path(project_root)))
    if overrides:
        data = _deep_merge(data, overrides)
    try:
        return ForgexConfig.model_validate(data)
    except ValidationError as exc:
        msg = f"Invalid configuration: {exc.error_count()} error(s): {exc}"
        raise ConfigError(msg) from exc


def save_config(config: ForgexConfig, path: Path) -> None:
    """Write a full config to *path* as TOML (None values omitted — TOML has no null)."""
    path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    text = tomli_w.dumps(config.model_dump(mode="json", exclude_none=True))
    path.write_text(text, encoding="utf-8")


def _coerce(raw: str) -> Any:
    """Convert a CLI string into bool/int/float when unambiguous."""
    if raw.lower() in ("true", "false"):
        return raw.lower() == "true"
    for cast in (int, float):

src/forgex/tools/git.py

python

"""Read-only Git helpers.

The checkpoint system (spec section 21) arrives in Phase 6. These queries
are safe, non-mutating, and used by `forgex status` and `forgex doctor`.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import subprocess
from pathlib import Path


def _git(args: list[str], cwd: Path) -> str | None:
    """Run a git query; return stripped stdout, or None on any failure."""
    try:
        proc = subprocess.run(
            ["git", *args],
            cwd=cwd,
            capture_output=True,
            text=True,
            timeout=15,
            check=False,
        )
    except (OSError, subprocess.TimeoutExpired):
        return None
    if proc.returncode != 0:
        return None
    return proc.stdout.strip()


def has_git_repo(root: Path) -> bool:
    """True if a .git entry exists (directory, or file for worktrees)."""
    return (root / ".git").exists()


def current_branch(root: Path) -> str | None:
    return _git(["rev-parse", "--abbrev-ref", "HEAD"], root)


def is_dirty(root: Path) -> bool | None:
    """True/False for clean/dirty; None when git is unusable."""
    out = _git(["status", "--porcelain"], root)
    if out is None:
        return None
    return bool(out)
src/forgex/project.py — the service layer behind init/status.

python

"""Project services: detection and initialization.

The CLI stays thin; future hosts (FastAPI server, web UI) call these
functions directly (spec section 60).
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import json
import tomllib
from dataclasses import asdict, dataclass, field
from datetime import UTC, datetime
from pathlib import Path

from forgex.schemas.error import ProjectError

FORGEX_DIR = ".forgex"

_PROJECT_CONFIG_TEMPLATE = """\
# FORGEX project configuration.
# Values here override ~/.forgex/config.toml — see `forgex config show`.

[workflow]
max_iterations = 5

[permissions]
mode = "ask"
"""


# --------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Detection
# --------------------------------------------------------------------------


@dataclass
class ProjectDetection:
    root: Path
    languages: list[str] = field(default_factory=list)
    package_managers: list[str] = field(default_factory=list)
    build_systems: list[str] = field(default_factory=list)
    test_frameworks: list[str] = field(default_factory=list)
    frameworks: list[str] = field(default_factory=list)
    has_git: bool = False

    def as_dict(self) -> dict:
        d = asdict(self)
        d["root"] = str(self.root)
        return d

"""Terminal output adapter.

src/forgex/cli/commands/init.py

python

"""`forgex init` — scaffold the .forgex directory and detect the project."""

from __future__ import annotations

from pathlib import Path
from typing import Annotated, Optional

import typer

from forgex.cli.ui import JsonFlag, PlainFlag, ui_from
from forgex.project import detect_project, initialize_project


def init_command(
    ctx: typer.Context,
    name: Annotated[Optional[str], typer.Argument(help="Target directory (defaults to the current one).")] = None,
    json_out: JsonFlag = False,
    plain: PlainFlag = False,
) -> None:
    """Initialize FORGEX in a project directory."""
    ui = ui_from(ctx, json_out=json_out, plain=plain)
    target = Path(name).resolve() if name else Path.cwd()

    root = initialize_project(target)  # raises ProjectError if already initialized
    detection = detect_project(root)

    ui.data({"root": str(root), "detection": detection.as_dict()})
    ui.ok(f"Initialized FORGEX project at {root}")
    ui.text(f"Detected: {detection.summary()}")
    if detection.has_git:
        ui.text("Git repository detected — checkpoints will be available.")
    else:
        ui.warn("No Git repository detected. Run 'git init' to enable checkpoints.")

src/forgex/cli/commands/status.py

python

"""`forgex status` — project detection, git state and FORGEX health."""
from forgex.cli.ui import JsonFlag, Output, PlainFlag, ui_from
from forgex.config import load_config
from forgex.project import detect_project
from forgex.schemas.error import ProjectError
from forgex.tools.git import current_branch, is_dirty
from forgex.utils.paths import find_project_root


def status_command(
    ctx: typer.Context,
    json_out: JsonFlag = False,
    plain: PlainFlag = False,
) -> None:
    """Show project status."""
    ui = ui_from(ctx, json_out=json_out, plain=plain)
    root = find_project_root()
    if root is None:
        raise ProjectError("Not inside a FORGEX project — run `forgex init` first.")

    config = load_config(root)
    detection = detect_project(root)
    branch = current_branch(root)
    dirty = is_dirty(root) if branch else None

    events_path = root / ".forgex" / "events.jsonl"
    n_events = sum(1 for line in events_path.read_text(encoding="utf-8").splitlines() if line.strip()) \
        if events_path.is_file() else 0
    n_checkpoints = len(list((root / ".forgex" / "checkpoints").glob("*")))

    git_state = (
        f"{branch} ({'dirty' if dirty else 'clean'})" if branch else "not a git repository"
    )
    payload = {
        "project": root.name,
        "root": str(root),
        "detection": detection.as_dict(),
        "git": {"branch": branch, "dirty": dirty},
        "checkpoints": n_checkpoints,
        "events": n_events,
        "workflow": {"max_iterations": config.workflow.max_iterations},
        "permissions": {"mode": config.permissions.mode},
    }
    ui.data(payload)

    lines = [
        f"Detected:    {detection.summary()}",
        f"Git:         {git_state}",
        f"Checkpoints: {n_checkpoints}",
        f"Events:      {n_events}",
        f"Workflow:    max_iterations={config.workflow.max_iterations}",
        f"Permissions: {config.permissions.mode}",
    ]
    if ui.mode is Output.RICH:
        ui.console.print(Panel("\n".join(lines), title=f"FORGEX — {root.name}", expand=False))
    elif ui.mode is Output.PLAIN:
        for line in lines:
            ui.console.print(line)
src/forgex/cli/commands/config.py

python

"""`forgex config` — view and modify layered configuration.

`config setup` (interactive) is intentionally NOT registered yet: it needs
provider knowledge that arrives in Phase 3. No fake commands (spec section 66).
"""

from __future__ import annotations

from typing import Annotated, Any

import typer

from forgex.cli.ui import JsonFlag, PlainFlag, ui_from
from forgex.config import load_config
from forgex.config.loader import global_config_path, project_config_path, set_config_value
from forgex.schemas.error import ConfigError
from forgex.utils.paths import find_project_root

app = typer.Typer(no_args_is_help=True, help="View and modify FORGEX configuration.")

GlobalFlag = Annotated[bool, typer.Option("--global", help="Operate on ~/.forgex/config.toml instead of the project config.")]


def _target_path(global_: bool) -> Any:
    if global_:
        return global_config_path()
    root = find_project_root()
    if root is None:
        msg = "Not inside a FORGEX project. Use --global to edit global configuration."
        raise ConfigError(msg)
    return project_config_path(root)


def _get_dotted(data: dict[str, Any], dotted: str) -> Any:
    node: Any = data
    for part in dotted.split("."):
src/forgex/cli/app.py

python

"""Typer application assembly and the controlled-error entry point.
from __future__ import annotations

import os
from typing import Annotated, Optional

import typer
from rich.console import Console

from forgex import __version__
from forgex.cli.commands import config as config_cmd
from forgex.cli.commands import doctor as doctor_cmd
from forgex.cli.commands import init as init_cmd
from forgex.cli.commands import status as status_cmd
from forgex.schemas.error import ForgexError
from forgex.utils.logging import configure_logging

app = typer.Typer(
    name="forgex",
    help="FORGEX — multi-agent AI software engineering. Plan. Build. Refine. Verify.",
    no_args_is_help=True,
    add_completion=False,
    context_settings={"help_option_names": ["-h", "--help"]},
)


def _version_callback(value: bool) -> None:
    if value:
        typer.echo(f"forgex {__version__}")
        raise typer.Exit


@app.callback()
def root(
    ctx: typer.Context,
    version: Annotated[Optional[bool], typer.Option("--version", callback=_version_callback, is_eager=True, help="Show version and exit.")] = None,
    verbose: Annotated[bool, typer.Option("--verbose", "-v", help="Verbose logging.")] = False,
    quiet: Annotated[bool, typer.Option("--quiet", help="Suppress non-essential output.")] = False,
    json_out: Annotated[bool, typer.Option("--json", help="Machine-readable JSON output.")] = False,
    plain: Annotated[bool, typer.Option("--plain", help="Plain output (CI/SSH safe).")] = False,
    debug: Annotated[bool, typer.Option("--debug", help="Show full tracebacks on errors.")] = False,
) -> None:
    """FORGEX — an AI software engineering team for your terminal."""
    if debug:
        os.environ["FORGEX_DEBUG"] = "1"
    configure_logging(level="debug" if verbose else "warning")
    ctx.obj = {"verbose": verbose, "quiet": quiet, "json": json_out, "plain": plain}


app.command(name="init", help="Initialize FORGEX in a project directory.")(init_cmd.init_command)
app.command(name="doctor", help="Check environment, tools and configuration.")(doctor_cmd.doctor_command)
app.command(name="status", help="Show project status.")(status_cmd.status_command)
app.add_typer(config_cmd.app, name="config", help="View and modify configuration.")


def main() -> None:
    """Console-script entry point with controlled error handling (spec sections 28, 36)."""
    try:
        app()
    except ForgexError as exc:
        if os.environ.get("FORGEX_DEBUG"):
            raise
        Console(stderr=True).print(f"[red]✗[/red] {exc}")
        raise SystemExit(exc.exit_code) from None
    except KeyboardInterrupt:
        # Phase 1: nothing long-running yet; Phase 9 adds full graceful state saving.
        Console(stderr=True).print("[yellow]Interrupted — on-disk state is preserved.[/yellow]")
        raise SystemExit(130) from None

tests/conftest.py

python

"""Shared fixtures. Every test runs with an isolated HOME so the user's real
~/.forgex is never read or written, and no real credentials leak in."""

from __future__ import annotations

import pytest


@pytest.fixture(autouse=True)
def isolated_home(tmp_path: "pytest.TempPathFactory", monkeypatch: pytest.MonkeyPatch):
    home = tmp_path / "home"
    home.mkdir()
    monkeypatch.setenv("HOME", str(home))
    monkeypatch.setenv("USERPROFILE", str(home))  # Windows
    monkeypatch.delenv("FORGEX_GROQ_API_KEY", raising=False)
    monkeypatch.delenv("FORGEX_OPENAI_API_KEY", raising=False)
    return home
tests/unit/test_paths.py

python

from __future__ import annotations

import sys
from pathlib import Path

import pytest

from forgex.utils.paths import PathViolationError, resolve_in_workspace


def test_relative_path_inside(tmp_path: Path):
    assert resolve_in_workspace(tmp_path, "src/main.py") == (tmp_path / "src" / "main.py").resolve()


def test_dotdot_still_inside_ok(tmp_path: Path):
    assert resolve_in_workspace(tmp_path, "src/../README.md") == (tmp_path / "README.md").resolve()


def test_dotdot_escape_rejected(tmp_path: Path):
    with pytest.raises(PathViolationError):
        resolve_in_workspace(tmp_path, "../outside.txt")


def test_absolute_outside_rejected(tmp_path: Path):
    with pytest.raises(PathViolationError):
        resolve_in_workspace(tmp_path, "/etc/passwd" if sys.platform != "win32" else "C:/Windows/system32")


def test_absolute_inside_allowed(tmp_path: Path):
    p = tmp_path / "a.txt"
    p.write_text("x")
    assert resolve_in_workspace(tmp_path, str(p)) == p.resolve()


def test_workspace_root_itself_allowed(tmp_path: Path):
    assert resolve_in_workspace(tmp_path, ".") == tmp_path.resolve()


@pytest.mark.skipif(sys.platform == "win32", reason="symlink creation needs privileges on Windows")
def test_symlink_escape_rejected(tmp_path: Path):
    outside = tmp_path.parent / "outside-dir"
    outside.mkdir(exist_ok=True)
    link = tmp_path / "link"
    link.symlink_to(outside, target_is_directory=True)
    with pytest.raises(PathViolationError):
        resolve_in_workspace(tmp_path, "link/secret.txt")
tests/unit/test_config.py

python

from __future__ import annotations

from pathlib import Path

import pytest

from forgex.config import load_config
from forgex.config.loader import set_config_value
from forgex.schemas.error import ConfigError


def test_defaults(isolated_home):
    cfg = load_config()
    assert cfg.workflow.max_iterations == 5
    assert cfg.permissions.mode == "ask"
    assert cfg.providers["groq"].api_key_env == "FORGEX_GROQ_API_KEY"


def test_project_overrides_global(isolated_home, tmp_path: Path):
    gdir = isolated_home / ".forgex"
    gdir.mkdir()
    (gdir / "config.toml").write_text("[workflow]\nmax_iterations = 3\n")
    proj = tmp_path / "proj" / ".forgex"
    proj.mkdir(parents=True)
    (proj / "config.toml").write_text("[workflow]\nmax_iterations = 7\n")
    assert load_config(tmp_path / "proj").workflow.max_iterations == 7


def test_invalid_value_raises(isolated_home, tmp_path: Path):
    pdir = tmp_path / "p" / ".forgex"
    pdir.mkdir(parents=True)
    (pdir / "config.toml").write_text('[workflow]\nmax_iterations = "nope"\n')
    with pytest.raises(ConfigError):
        load_config(tmp_path / "p")


def test_unknown_key_rejected(isolated_home, tmp_path: Path):
    pdir = tmp_path / "p" / ".forgex"
    pdir.mkdir(parents=True)
    (pdir / "config.toml").write_text("[workflog]\nmax_iterations = 3\n")  # typo
    with pytest.raises(ConfigError):
        load_config(tmp_path / "p")


def test_set_config_value_roundtrip(isolated_home, tmp_path: Path):
    path = tmp_path / ".forgex" / "config.toml"
    set_config_value(path, "models.architect.model", "llama-3.3-70b-versatile")
    set_config_value(path, "workflow.max_iterations", "8")
    set_config_value(path, "sandbox.enabled", "true")
    cfg = load_config(tmp_path)
    assert cfg.models.architect.model == "llama-3.3-70b-versatile"
    assert cfg.workflow.max_iterations == 8
    assert cfg.sandbox.enabled is True


def test_set_invalid_value_refused(isolated_home, tmp_path: Path):
    path = tmp_path / ".forgex" / "config.toml"
    with pytest.raises(ConfigError):
        set_config_value(path, "workflow.max_iterations", "999")
    assert not path.exists()  # nothing written on validation failure
tests/unit/test_project.py

python

from __future__ import annotations

from pathlib import Path

from forgex.project import detect_project


def test_empty_dir(tmp_path: Path):
    det = detect_project(tmp_path)
    assert det.languages == []
    assert det.has_git is False


def test_python_fastapi(tmp_path: Path):
    (tmp_path / "pyproject.toml").write_text(
        '[project]\ndependencies = ["fastapi>=0.111", "sqlalchemy"]\n'
    )
    (tmp_path / "requirements.txt").write_text("pytest\n")
    det = detect_project(tmp_path)
    assert "Python" in det.languages
    assert "FastAPI" in det.frameworks
    assert "SQLAlchemy" in det.frameworks
    assert "Pytest" in det.test_frameworks
    assert "pip" in det.package_managers


def test_node_pnpm(tmp_path: Path):
    (tmp_path / "package.json").write_text(
        '{"dependencies": {"express": "^4"}, "devDependencies": {"jest": "^29"}}'
    )
    (tmp_path / "pnpm-lock.yaml").write_text("")
    det = detect_project(tmp_path)
    assert "JavaScript" in det.languages
    assert "Express" in det.frameworks
    assert "Jest" in det.test_frameworks
    assert "pnpm" in det.package_managers


def test_rust_and_go(tmp_path: Path):
    (tmp_path / "Cargo.toml").write_text("[package]\nname = 'x'\n")
    det = detect_project(tmp_path)
    assert "Rust" in det.languages and "cargo" in det.package_managers

    (tmp_path / "go.mod").write_text("module example.com/x\n\ngo 1.22\n")
    det = detect_project(tmp_path)
    assert "Go" in det.languages
tests/unit/test_init.py

python

from __future__ import annotations

from pathlib import Path

import pytest

from forgex.project import initialize_project
from forgex.schemas.error import ProjectError


def test_creates_scaffold(tmp_path: Path):
    root = initialize_project(tmp_path / "proj")
    fx = root / ".forgex"
    assert (fx / "config.toml").is_file()
    assert (fx / "events.jsonl").is_file()
    assert (fx / "checkpoints").is_dir()
    assert (fx / "cache").is_dir()
    assert (fx / ".gitignore").read_text().strip() == "cache/"
    lines = (fx / "events.jsonl").read_text().strip().splitlines()
    assert len(lines) == 1 and "ProjectInitialized" in lines[0]


def test_refuses_overwrite(tmp_path: Path):
    initialize_project(tmp_path)
    with pytest.raises(ProjectError):
        initialize_project(tmp_path)


def test_rejects_file_target(tmp_path: Path):
    f = tmp_path / "afile"
    f.write_text("x")
    with pytest.raises(ProjectError):
        initialize_project(f)
tests/unit/test_cli.py

python

from __future__ import annotations

import json
from pathlib import Path

from typer.testing import CliRunner

from forgex import __version__
from forgex.cli.app import app

runner = CliRunner()


def test_help():
    result = runner.invoke(app, ["--help"])
    assert result.exit_code == 0
    assert "FORGEX" in result.output


def test_version():
    result = runner.invoke(app, ["--version"])
    assert result.exit_code == 0
    assert __version__ in result.output


def test_doctor_json(tmp_path: Path, monkeypatch):
    monkeypatch.chdir(tmp_path)
    result = runner.invoke(app, ["doctor", "--json"])
    assert result.exit_code == 0
    payload = json.loads(result.output)
    assert payload["ok"] is True
    names = [c["name"] for c in payload["checks"]]
    assert "Python" in names and "SQLite" in names


def test_init_then_status(tmp_path: Path, monkeypatch):
    monkeypatch.chdir(tmp_path)
    r1 = runner.invoke(app, ["init"])
    assert r1.exit_code == 0
    r2 = runner.invoke(app, ["status", "--json"])
    assert r2.exit_code == 0
    payload = json.loads(r2.output)
    assert payload["project"] == tmp_path.name
    assert payload["events"] >= 1


def test_status_outside_project_fails(tmp_path: Path, monkeypatch):
    monkeypatch.chdir(tmp_path)
    result = runner.invoke(app, ["status"])
    assert result.exit_code == 4  # ProjectError


def test_config_set_get(tmp_path: Path, monkeypatch):
    monkeypatch.chdir(tmp_path)
    runner.invoke(app, ["init"])
    r = runner.invoke(app, ["config", "set", "workflow.max_iterations", "9"])
    assert r.exit_code == 0
    r = runner.invoke(app, ["config", "get", "workflow.max_iterations", "--json"])
    assert r.exit_code == 0
    assert json.loads(r.output)["value"] == 9
README.md

FORGEX
Plan. Build. Refine. Verify.

FORGEX is a CLI-first autonomous AI software engineering system: multiplespecialized agents (Architect, Builder, Expert Coder, Supervisor) collaborateon a real repository through controlled tools, Git checkpoints, testing, anda self-healing repair loop.

Status: Alpha — Phase 1 of 15. The CLI foundation, layeredconfiguration, project detection, init/doctor/status/config worktoday. Agents and providers arrive in Phases 3–9. Nothing is faked: whatisn't implemented yet simply isn't registered as a command.

Requirements
Python 3.12+
Git (required from Phase 6 for checkpoints; optional now)
Docker, ripgrep — optional
Install (development)
git clone <your-repo> forgex && cd forgexpython3 -m venv .venv && source .venv/bin/activatepip install -e ".[dev]"
Quick start
forgex --helpforgex doctor            # verify your environmentmkdir demo && cd demoforgex init              # scaffold .forgex/, detect your projectforgex status            # project + git + config overview
Configuration
Layered (highest wins): CLI args → environment → project .forgex/config.toml→ global ~/.forgex/config.toml → defaults. Secrets live only inenvironment variables — never in config files, logs, or git:

export FORGEX_GROQ_API_KEY=...export FORGEX_OPENAI_API_KEY=...
forgex config showforgex config set models.architect.model llama-3.3-70b-versatileforgex config set workflow.max_iterations 8 --global
Development
pytest            # test suiteruff check .      # lintmypy src          # types
Roadmap
See docs/architecture.md for the full phase plan. Next: Phase 2 —ProjectState + SQLite storage + event system.

(Create LICENSE with the standard MIT text, copyright "FORGEX Contributors".)

PHASE 1 REPORT
PHASE COMPLETED: Phase 1 — CLI Foundation (pending your verification)

IMPLEMENTED:

Typer CLI with --help, --version, --json/--plain/--quiet/--verbose/--debug output modes (usable before or after subcommands)
Layered config (defaults → global → project → overrides) with Pydantic validation, extra="forbid", and write-validated config set/get/show
forgex init — .forgex/ scaffolding (config.toml, events.jsonl, checkpoints/, cache/, inner .gitignore), never overwrites, emits first event
forgex doctor — 9 checks, required vs optional, --json, meaningful exit codes
forgex status — project detection (Python/JS/TS/Rust/Go/JVM manifests, frameworks, test frameworks, package managers), git branch/dirty, checkpoint/event counts
Error taxonomy (§28) + controlled entry point (ForgexError → clean message; traceback only with --debug; Ctrl+C → exit 130)
Security boundary #1 shipped: resolve_in_workspace() with traversal/absolute/symlink escape rejection — the gate every Phase 4 tool will use
FILES CHANGED: 25 files (listed above)

TESTS RUN: Not executed in this session — I don't have a runtime here, and per spec §80 I will not claim results I didn't observe. 24 tests provided.

TEST RESULTS: Awaiting your run:

bash

git init && pip install -e ".[dev]" && pytest -q
forgex --help && forgex doctor && forgex init demo && cd demo && forgex status
KNOWN ISSUES / LIMITS (honest list):

forgex config setup (interactive) is deferred to Phase 3 — it needs provider knowledge; a stub would violate §66.
tomli-w cannot serialize None — config show omits unset values (by design, documented).
Symlink-escape test is skipped on Windows (needs privileges); the guard itself is platform-agnostic.
Git is an optional doctor check until Phase 6 (checkpoints) — deliberate, keeps CI deterministic.
resolve_in_workspace has a theoretical TOCTOU window if a symlink is swapped between check and use — addressed in Phase 13 hardening.
NEXT PHASE: Phase 2 — ProjectState, TaskState, RunState, SQLite storage (repository abstraction), formal event system on the frozen JSONL format, .forgex/ state files, and their tests.

Your move: paste the output of pytest -q and forgex doctor (plus the environment check from §0.1). If everything is green, say "Phase 2" and I'll build the state + storage layer. If anything fails, paste the traceback and I'll fix it before moving on.


Phase 2 it is. If you haven't run the Phase 1 suite yet, no problem — the commands at the end run the entire suite (Phase 2 only touches init/status behavior, and their tests are updated accordingly).

PHASE 2 — PROJECT STATE + STORAGE
2.0 Design decisions (ADR-style additions to the Phase 0 log)
Dual persistence, one truth. JSON files in .forgex/ (state.json, tasks.json, events.jsonl) are the canonical, git-friendly, human-inspectable source of truth. SQLite (.forgex/state.db) is a rebuildable query index + run history. Every save writes JSON first, then mirrors to SQLite.
Interrupt safety (§36) starts here. All JSON writes are atomic (tmp file → fsync → os.replace); event appends are fsync'd. A Ctrl+C can never leave a half-written state file.
WAL mode on SQLite → concurrent readers safe, fast.
Forward-only migrations. v1 creates the 5 tables with Phase 2 consumers (projects, runs, tasks, events, errors). model_usage arrives as v2 with providers (Phase 3), checkpoints as v3 with the Git system (Phase 6) — no dead tables, no fake features.
Schemas = data, orchestration = behavior, storage = persistence. All Pydantic models live in schemas/; storage/ imports only schemas/ (never orchestration/) so the PostgreSQL swap-out later touches one package.
Degraded mode. open_stores(create_db=False) gives JSON-only stores — used by forgex status so a read-only command never creates a database.
New error classes: WorkflowError (14), TaskGraphError (15), StorageError (16).
2.1 Files — new ✚ / modified ✏

src/forgex/schemas/error.py ✏ (full replacement)

python

"""FORGEX error taxonomy and the persisted error record (spec sections 5, 25, 28).

All controlled failures derive from :class:`ForgexError` so the CLI can
render friendly messages and keep raw stack traces behind ``--debug``.
Exit codes are stable per error class.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

from datetime import UTC, datetime
from typing import Any

from pydantic import BaseModel, ConfigDict, Field


class ForgexError(Exception):
    """Base class for all controlled FORGEX errors."""

    exit_code: int = 1


class ConfigError(ForgexError):
    exit_code = 2


class FileSystemError(ForgexError):
    exit_code = 3


class ProjectError(ForgexError):
    exit_code = 4


class ProviderError(ForgexError):
    exit_code = 5


class AuthenticationError(ProviderError):
    exit_code = 6


class RateLimitError(ProviderError):
    exit_code = 7


class ToolError(ForgexError):
    exit_code = 8


class SandboxError(ForgexError):
    exit_code = 9


class GitError(ForgexError):
    exit_code = 10

src/forgex/schemas/event.py ✚

python

"""Event schema (spec section 24).

On-disk format (.forgex/events.jsonl), one JSON object per line:

    {"ts": "<iso-8601 utc>", "type": "<EventType>", "run_id": null, "data": {...}}

Introduced in Phase 1; ``run_id`` added in Phase 2 (readers tolerate its
absence). This format is FROZEN — treat it as a public contract.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import json
from datetime import UTC, datetime
from enum import Enum
from typing import Any

from pydantic import BaseModel, ConfigDict, Field


class EventType(str, Enum):
    PROJECT_INITIALIZED = "ProjectInitialized"
    RUN_STARTED = "RunStarted"
    RUN_COMPLETED = "RunCompleted"
    RUN_FAILED = "RunFailed"
    AGENT_STARTED = "AgentStarted"
    AGENT_THINKING = "AgentThinking"
    AGENT_COMPLETED = "AgentCompleted"
    TOOL_STARTED = "ToolStarted"
    TOOL_COMPLETED = "ToolCompleted"
    FILE_CHANGED = "FileChanged"
    TASK_STARTED = "TaskStarted"
    TASK_COMPLETED = "TaskCompleted"
    TEST_STARTED = "TestStarted"
    TEST_COMPLETED = "TestCompleted"
    SUPERVISOR_STARTED = "SupervisorStarted"
    REPAIR_STARTED = "RepairStarted"
    CHECKPOINT_CREATED = "CheckpointCreated"
    INTERRUPTED = "Interrupted"


class Event(BaseModel):
    """One immutable event in the FORGEX observability stream."""

    model_config = ConfigDict(extra="forbid")

    ts: datetime = Field(default_factory=lambda: datetime.now(UTC))
    type: EventType
    data: dict[str, Any] = Field(default_factory=dict)
    run_id: str | None = None

    def to_jsonl(self) -> str:
        return json.dumps(
            {"ts": self.ts.isoformat(), "type": self.type.value, "run_id": self.run_id, "data": self.data},
            default=str,
        )

    @classmethod
    def from_jsonl(cls, line: str) -> "Event":
        """Parse one JSONL line. Raises ValueError/KeyError on malformed input."""
        raw = json.loads(line)
        return cls(
            ts=datetime.fromisoformat(raw["ts"]),
            type=EventType(raw["type"]),
            run_id=raw.get("run_id"),
            data=raw.get("data") or {},
        )
src/forgex/schemas/task.py ✚

python

"""Task system (spec section 54).

Tasks support dependency ordering, priority, attempts, per-task files
and acceptance criteria. The graph operations (topological order,
readiness, cycle detection) live here so every consumer — engine, CLI,
tests — shares one implementation.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

from datetime import UTC, datetime
from enum import Enum

from pydantic import BaseModel, ConfigDict, Field

from forgex.schemas.error import TaskGraphError


class TaskStatus(str, Enum):
    PENDING = "pending"
    IN_PROGRESS = "in_progress"
    COMPLETED = "completed"
    FAILED = "failed"
    SKIPPED = "skipped"


class TaskPriority(str, Enum):
    LOW = "low"
    MEDIUM = "medium"
    HIGH = "high"
    CRITICAL = "critical"


_PRIORITY_RANK = {
    TaskPriority.LOW: 0,
    TaskPriority.MEDIUM: 1,
    TaskPriority.HIGH: 2,
    TaskPriority.CRITICAL: 3,
}


class Task(BaseModel):
    """A single unit of work produced by the Architect, executed by the Builder."""

    model_config = ConfigDict(extra="forbid")

    id: str  # e.g. "TASK-004"
    description: str
    priority: TaskPriority = TaskPriority.MEDIUM
    status: TaskStatus = TaskStatus.PENDING
    depends_on: list[str] = Field(default_factory=list)
    files: list[str] = Field(default_factory=list)
    acceptance_criteria: list[str] = Field(default_factory=list)
src/forgex/schemas/project.py ✚

python

"""Project and run state (spec sections 5, 53).

ProjectState is THE central shared object: agents read and write
structured state instead of giant conversations (spec section 2.7).
WorkflowStatus is the phase machine from spec section 53; the transition
RULES live in forgex.orchestration.workflow.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

from datetime import UTC, datetime
from enum import Enum

from pydantic import BaseModel, ConfigDict, Field

from forgex.schemas.agent import AgentRole
from forgex.schemas.error import ErrorRecord


class WorkflowStatus(str, Enum):
    INITIALIZING = "initializing"
    ANALYZING = "analyzing"
    RESEARCHING = "researching"
    PLANNING = "planning"
    BUILDING = "building"
    REVIEWING = "reviewing"
    TESTING = "testing"
    SUPERVISING = "supervising"
    REPAIRING = "repairing"
    COMPLETED = "completed"
    FAILED = "failed"
    CANCELLED = "cancelled"


class RunStatus(str, Enum):
    RUNNING = "running"
    COMPLETED = "completed"
    FAILED = "failed"
    CANCELLED = "cancelled"


class ProjectState(BaseModel):
    """Durable project state, persisted to .forgex/state.json (spec section 5)."""

    model_config = ConfigDict(extra="forbid")

    schema_version: int = 1
    project_id: str
    project_path: str
    created_at: datetime = Field(default_factory=lambda: datetime.now(UTC))
    updated_at: datetime = Field(default_factory=lambda: datetime.now(UTC))

    user_request: str | None = None

src/forgex/orchestration/workflow.py ✚

python

"""Workflow state machine (spec sections 12, 53).

Transitions are explicit; nothing may skip phases or leave a terminal
state. The repair loop is a first-class path: TESTING and SUPERVISING
can both fall into REPAIRING, which returns to REVIEWING/TESTING/BUILDING.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

from forgex.schemas.error import WorkflowError
from forgex.schemas.project import WorkflowStatus

ALLOWED_TRANSITIONS: dict[WorkflowStatus, frozenset[WorkflowStatus]] = {
    WorkflowStatus.INITIALIZING: frozenset(
        {WorkflowStatus.ANALYZING, WorkflowStatus.FAILED, WorkflowStatus.CANCELLED}
    ),
    WorkflowStatus.ANALYZING: frozenset(
        {WorkflowStatus.RESEARCHING, WorkflowStatus.PLANNING, WorkflowStatus.FAILED, WorkflowStatus.CANCELLED}
    ),
    WorkflowStatus.RESEARCHING: frozenset(
        {WorkflowStatus.PLANNING, WorkflowStatus.FAILED, WorkflowStatus.CANCELLED}
    ),
    WorkflowStatus.PLANNING: frozenset(
        {WorkflowStatus.BUILDING, WorkflowStatus.FAILED, WorkflowStatus.CANCELLED}
    ),
    WorkflowStatus.BUILDING: frozenset(
        {WorkflowStatus.REVIEWING, WorkflowStatus.TESTING, WorkflowStatus.FAILED, WorkflowStatus.CANCELLED}
    ),
    WorkflowStatus.REVIEWING: frozenset(
        {WorkflowStatus.TESTING, WorkflowStatus.REPAIRING, WorkflowStatus.FAILED, WorkflowStatus.CANCELLED}
    ),
    WorkflowStatus.TESTING: frozenset(
        {WorkflowStatus.SUPERVISING, WorkflowStatus.REPAIRING, WorkflowStatus.FAILED, WorkflowStatus.CANCELLED}
    ),
    WorkflowStatus.SUPERVISING: frozenset(
        {WorkflowStatus.COMPLETED, WorkflowStatus.REPAIRING, WorkflowStatus.FAILED, WorkflowStatus.CANCELLED}
    ),
    WorkflowStatus.REPAIRING: frozenset(
        {WorkflowStatus.REVIEWING, WorkflowStatus.TESTING, WorkflowStatus.BUILDING, WorkflowStatus.FAILED, WorkflowStatus.CANCELLED}
    ),
    WorkflowStatus.COMPLETED: frozenset(),
    WorkflowStatus.FAILED: frozenset(),
    WorkflowStatus.CANCELLED: frozenset(),
}


def can_transition(current: WorkflowStatus, new: WorkflowStatus) -> bool:
    return new in ALLOWED_TRANSITIONS[current]


def transition(current: WorkflowStatus, new: WorkflowStatus) -> WorkflowStatus:
    """Validate a transition and return the new status.

    Raises WorkflowError on illegal transitions — a hard guard against the
    workflow engine ever entering an undefined state.
    """
    if new not in ALLOWED_TRANSITIONS[current]:
        msg = f"Illegal workflow transition: {current.value} -> {new.value}"
        raise WorkflowError(msg)
    return new
src/forgex/orchestration/state.py ✚

python

"""Durable project state persistence (spec sections 5, 6, 36).

JSON files under .forgex/ are the canonical, human-inspectable,
git-friendly source of truth. Writes are atomic (tmp + fsync + rename)
so an interrupt can never corrupt state (spec section 36).
SQLite mirroring is handled by ProjectStores (orchestration/stores.py).
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import json
import os
from datetime import UTC, datetime
from pathlib import Path

from pydantic import ValidationError

from forgex.schemas.error import ProjectError
from forgex.schemas.project import ProjectState
from forgex.schemas.task import Task

STATE_FILE = "state.json"
TASKS_FILE = "tasks.json"


def _atomic_write_json(path: Path, payload: dict) -> None:
    """Write JSON atomically: temp file, fsync, rename (spec section 36)."""
    tmp = path.with_name(path.name + ".tmp")
    with tmp.open("w", encoding="utf-8") as fh:
        json.dump(payload, fh, indent=2, default=str)
        fh.flush()
        os.fsync(fh.fileno())
    os.replace(tmp, path)


class StateStore:
    """Reads/writes .forgex/state.json."""

    def __init__(self, project_root: Path) -> None:
        self._dir = Path(project_root) / ".forgex"

    def path(self) -> Path:
        return self._dir / STATE_FILE

    def load(self) -> ProjectState | None:
        """Load state; None when no state file exists yet.

        Raises ProjectError (not a raw traceback) if the file is corrupt.
        """
        state_path = self.path()
src/forgex/orchestration/events.py ✚

python

"""Event system (spec section 24).
            if callback in self._subscribers:
                self._subscribers.remove(callback)

        return _unsubscribe

    def record(
        self,
        type_: EventType,
        data: dict | None = None,
        *,
        run_id: str | None = None,
    ) -> Event:
        """Append one event (durable first), index it, notify subscribers."""
        event = Event(
            type=type_,
            data=data or {},
            run_id=run_id or self.default_run_id,
        )
        # 1. Durable append (fsync — interrupt-safe, spec section 36).
        self._path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
        with self._path.open("a", encoding="utf-8") as fh:
            fh.write(event.to_jsonl() + "\n")
            fh.flush()
            os.fsync(fh.fileno())
        # 2. Query index (best-effort — rebuildable).
        if self._repo is not None:
            try:
                self._repo.insert(event)
            except Exception:  # noqa: BLE001 — index failure must never kill a run
                logger.warning("Event SQLite index write failed for %s", event.type.value, exc_info=True)
        # 3. Subscribers (a broken subscriber must never kill a run).
        for callback in list(self._subscribers):
            try:
                callback(event)
            except Exception:  # noqa: BLE001
                logger.warning("Event subscriber raised; continuing", exc_info=True)
        return event


def read_events(
    path: Path,
    *,
    limit: int | None = None,
    event_type: EventType | None = None,
) -> list[Event]:
    """Read events from a JSONL file.

    Malformed lines are skipped with a warning (an interrupted append can
    leave a partial final line). `limit` returns the LAST n events.
    """
    if not path.is_file():
        return []
    events: list[Event] = []
    for line in path.read_text(encoding="utf-8").splitlines():
        line = line.strip()
        if not line:
            continue
        try:
            event = Event.from_jsonl(line)
        except (KeyError, ValueError):
            logger.warning("Skipping malformed event line in %s", path)
            continue
        if event_type is not None and event.type is not event_type:
            continue
        events.append(event)
    if limit is not None:
        return events[-limit:] if limit > 0 else []
    return events
src/forgex/orchestration/stores.py ✚

python

"""Project persistence bundle — the dependency-injection point.

`open_stores()` gives every service (now, and the engine in Phase 9) a
single handle to all durable storage for one project:

  * StateStore / TaskStore — canonical JSON files in .forgex/
  * EventRecorder          — durable events.jsonl + SQLite index
  * repositories           — SQLite query index + run history

JSON files are the source of truth; the SQLite index is rebuildable.
With `create_db=False` the stores run in degraded JSON-only mode and
never create a database (used by read-only commands like `status`).
"""

from __future__ import annotations

from collections.abc import Iterator
from contextlib import contextmanager
from pathlib import Path

from forgex.orchestration.events import EventRecorder
from forgex.orchestration.state import StateStore, TaskStore
from forgex.schemas.error import ProjectError
from forgex.schemas.project import ProjectState
from forgex.schemas.task import Task
from forgex.storage.database import Database
from forgex.storage.repositories import (
    ErrorRepository,
    EventRepository,
    ProjectRepository,
    RunRepository,
    TaskRepository,
)

DB_FILE_NAME = "state.db"


class ProjectStores:
    """All persistence handles for one project.

    `db` and every repository attribute are None in degraded (JSON-only)
    mode; `save_state`/`save_tasks` degrade gracefully.
    """

    def __init__(self, project_root: Path, db: Database | None) -> None:
        self.root = Path(project_root)
        self.forgex_dir = self.root / ".forgex"
        self.db = db
        self.state = StateStore(self.root)
        self.tasks = TaskStore(self.root)
        self.projects = ProjectRepository(db) if db else None
        self.runs = RunRepository(db) if db else None
        self.task_repo = TaskRepository(db) if db else None
        self.events_repo = EventRepository(db) if db else None
        self.errors = ErrorRepository(db) if db else None
        self.recorder = EventRecorder(self.forgex_dir / "events.jsonl", self.events_repo)

    def save_state(self, state: ProjectState) -> None:
        """Canonical save: durable JSON first, then mirror to the SQLite index."""
        self.state.save(state)
        if self.projects is not None:
            self.projects.upsert(state)

src/forgex/storage/database.py ✚

python

"""SQLite access layer (spec section 25).

The project database lives at .forgex/state.db (gitignored): a
rebuildable query index and run history. WAL mode keeps concurrent
readers safe; foreign keys are enforced.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import sqlite3
from pathlib import Path

from forgex.storage.migrations import run_migrations


class Database:
    """Lazy-connecting wrapper around a project-local SQLite database."""

    def __init__(self, path: Path) -> None:
        self.path = Path(path)
        self._conn: sqlite3.Connection | None = None

    @property
    def conn(self) -> sqlite3.Connection:
        if self._conn is None:
            self.path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
            conn = sqlite3.connect(self.path)
            conn.row_factory = sqlite3.Row
            conn.execute("PRAGMA foreign_keys = ON")
            conn.execute("PRAGMA journal_mode = WAL")
            conn.execute("PRAGMA synchronous = NORMAL")
            conn.execute("PRAGMA busy_timeout = 5000")
            run_migrations(conn)
            self._conn = conn
        return self._conn

    def close(self) -> None:
        if self._conn is not None:
            self._conn.close()
            self._conn = None

                (
                    state.project_id,

                    Path(state.project_path).name,

"""`forgex logs` — inspect the project event stream (spec section 24)."""

from __future__ import annotations

import os
import time
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Annotated, Optional

import typer
from rich.markup import escape

from forgex.cli.ui import JsonFlag, Output, PlainFlag, ui_from
from forgex.orchestration.events import read_events
from forgex.schemas.error import ConfigError, ProjectError
from forgex.schemas.event import Event, EventType
from forgex.utils.paths import find_project_root


def _pairs(event: Event, width: int = 60) -> str:
    parts = []
    for key, value in event.data.items():
        text = str(value).replace("\n", " ")
        if len(text) > width:
            text = text[: width - 1] + "…"
        parts.append(f"{key}={text}")
    return " ".join(parts)


def _render(event: Event, *, rich: bool) -> str:
    ts = event.ts.strftime("%H:%M:%S")
    run = f" run={event.run_id[:8]}" if event.run_id else ""
    pairs = _pairs(event)
    if rich:
        return (
            f"[dim]{ts}[/dim] [bold cyan]{event.type.value}[/bold cyan]"
            f"[dim]{escape(run)} {escape(pairs)}[/dim]"
        ).rstrip()
    return f"{ts} {event.type.value}{run} {pairs}".rstrip()


def _follow(events_path: Path, event_type: EventType | None, ui) -> None:
    """Poll the JSONL file and print appended events until interrupted.
    KeyboardInterrupt propagates to the CLI entry point (clean exit 130)."""
    with events_path.open("r", encoding="utf-8") as fh:
        fh.seek(0, os.SEEK_END)
        while True:
            line = fh.readline()
            if not line:
                time.sleep(0.5)
                continue
            line = line.strip()
            if not line:
src/forgex/cli/commands/init.py ✏ (full replacement)

"""`forgex init` — scaffold the .forgex directory and detect the project."""

from __future__ import annotations

from pathlib import Path
from typing import Annotated, Optional

import typer

from forgex.cli.ui import JsonFlag, PlainFlag, ui_from
from forgex.orchestration.state import StateStore
from forgex.project import detect_project, initialize_project


def init_command(
    ctx: typer.Context,
    name: Annotated[Optional[str], typer.Argument(help="Target directory (defaults to the current one).")] = None,
    json_out: JsonFlag = False,
    plain: PlainFlag = False,
) -> None:
    """Initialize FORGEX in a project directory."""
    ui = ui_from(ctx, json_out=json_out, plain=plain)
    target = Path(name).resolve() if name else Path.cwd()

    root = initialize_project(target)  # raises ProjectError if already initialized
    detection = detect_project(root)
    state = StateStore(root).load()

    ui.data({
        "root": str(root),
        "project_id": state.project_id if state else None,
        "detection": detection.as_dict(),
    })
    ui.ok(f"Initialized FORGEX project at {root}")
    if state:
        ui.text(f"Project ID: {state.project_id[:8]}")
    ui.text(f"Detected: {detection.summary()}")
    if detection.has_git:
        ui.text("Git repository detected — checkpoints will be available.")
    else:
        ui.warn("No Git repository detected. Run 'git init' to enable checkpoints.")

        f"Tasks:       {task_counts['completed']}/{len(tasks)} completed"

    )
    lines += [
        f"Checkpoints: {n_checkpoints}",
        f"Events:      {n_events}",

tests/unit/test_workflow.py ✚

python

from __future__ import annotations

import pytest

from forgex.orchestration.workflow import ALLOWED_TRANSITIONS, can_transition, transition
from forgex.schemas.error import WorkflowError
from forgex.schemas.project import WorkflowStatus as WS


def test_happy_path_chain():
    chain = [
        WS.INITIALIZING, WS.ANALYZING, WS.RESEARCHING, WS.PLANNING, WS.BUILDING,
        WS.REVIEWING, WS.TESTING, WS.SUPERVISING, WS.COMPLETED,
    ]
    current = chain[0]
    for nxt in chain[1:]:
        current = transition(current, nxt)
    assert current is WS.COMPLETED


def test_repair_loop_transitions():
    assert can_transition(WS.TESTING, WS.REPAIRING)
    assert can_transition(WS.SUPERVISING, WS.REPAIRING)
    assert can_transition(WS.REPAIRING, WS.TESTING)
    assert can_transition(WS.REPAIRING, WS.BUILDING)


def test_invalid_transition_rejected():
    with pytest.raises(WorkflowError):
        transition(WS.PLANNING, WS.TESTING)


def test_terminal_states_locked():
    for terminal in (WS.COMPLETED, WS.FAILED, WS.CANCELLED):
        assert ALLOWED_TRANSITIONS[terminal] == frozenset()
        with pytest.raises(WorkflowError):
            transition(terminal, WS.BUILDING)


def test_every_active_state_can_fail_or_cancel():
    terminals = (WS.COMPLETED, WS.FAILED, WS.CANCELLED)
    for status in WS:
        if status in terminals:
            continue
        assert WS.CANCELLED in ALLOWED_TRANSITIONS[status], status
        assert WS.FAILED in ALLOWED_TRANSITIONS[status], status

from __future__ import annotations

import json
from datetime import UTC, datetime

from forgex.orchestration.events import EventRecorder, read_events
from forgex.schemas.event import Event, EventType
from forgex.storage.database import Database
from forgex.storage.repositories import EventRepository


def test_record_appends_jsonl(tmp_path):
    recorder = EventRecorder(tmp_path / "events.jsonl")
    event = recorder.record(EventType.RUN_STARTED, {"request": "demo"})
    lines = (tmp_path / "events.jsonl").read_text().strip().splitlines()
    assert len(lines) == 1
    raw = json.loads(lines[0])
    assert raw["type"] == "RunStarted"
    assert raw["data"] == {"request": "demo"}
    assert raw["run_id"] is None
    assert event.type is EventType.RUN_STARTED


def test_record_persists_to_sqlite(tmp_path):
    db = Database(tmp_path / "state.db")
    repo = EventRepository(db)
    recorder = EventRecorder(tmp_path / "events.jsonl", repository=repo)
    recorder.record(EventType.TASK_COMPLETED, {"task": "TASK-001"}, run_id="run-abc")
    assert repo.count() == 1
    recent = repo.recent(limit=10)
    assert recent[0].type is EventType.TASK_COMPLETED
    assert recent[0].run_id == "run-abc"
    db.close()


def test_subscribers_notified_then_unsubscribed(tmp_path):
    recorder = EventRecorder(tmp_path / "events.jsonl")
    seen: list[Event] = []
    unsubscribe = recorder.subscribe(seen.append)
    recorder.record(EventType.AGENT_STARTED, {"agent": "architect"})
    unsubscribe()
    recorder.record(EventType.AGENT_STARTED, {"agent": "builder"})
    assert [e.data["agent"] for e in seen] == ["architect"]


def test_broken_subscriber_does_not_break_recording(tmp_path):
    recorder = EventRecorder(tmp_path / "events.jsonl")

    def bad(_event: Event) -> None:
        raise RuntimeError("boom")

    recorder.subscribe(bad)
    recorder.record(EventType.FILE_CHANGED, {"path": "x"})
    assert len(read_events(tmp_path / "events.jsonl")) == 1


def test_read_events_limit_and_filter(tmp_path):
    path = tmp_path / "events.jsonl"
    recorder = EventRecorder(path)
    for i in range(5):
        etype = EventType.AGENT_STARTED if i % 2 == 0 else EventType.TOOL_STARTED
        recorder.record(etype, {"i": i})
    assert len(read_events(path)) == 5
    last_two = read_events(path, limit=2)

from __future__ import annotations

import pytest

from forgex.orchestration.state import StateStore, TaskStore
from forgex.schemas.error import ProjectError
from forgex.schemas.project import ProjectState

def test_state_roundtrip(tmp_path):
    store = StateStore(tmp_path)
    state = ProjectState(project_id="abc123", project_path=str(tmp_path), user_request="todo api")
    state.changed_files.append("src/main.py")
    store.save(state)
    loaded = store.load()
    assert loaded is not None
    assert loaded.project_id == "abc123"
    assert loaded.user_request == "todo api"
    assert loaded.changed_files == ["src/main.py"]


def test_save_bumps_updated_at(tmp_path):
    store = StateStore(tmp_path)
    state = ProjectState(project_id="x", project_path=str(tmp_path))
    before = state.updated_at
    store.save(state)
    assert state.updated_at >= before
    assert store.load().updated_at == state.updated_at


def test_load_missing_returns_none(tmp_path):
    assert StateStore(tmp_path).load() is None


def test_atomic_write_leaves_no_tmp(tmp_path):
    StateStore(tmp_path).save(ProjectState(project_id="x", project_path=str(tmp_path)))
    assert (tmp_path / ".forgex" / "state.json").is_file()
    assert not list((tmp_path / ".forgex").glob("*.tmp"))

        StateStore(tmp_path).load()

    assert store.load() == []

        Task(id="TASK-001", description="Scaffold API", priority=TaskPriority.HIGH),
        Task(id="TASK-002", description="Add tests", depends_on=["TASK-001"],
             acceptance_criteria=["pytest passes"]),

tests/unit/test_tasks.py ✚

python

from __future__ import annotations

import pytest

from forgex.schemas.error import TaskGraphError
from forgex.schemas.task import Task, TaskPriority, TaskStatus, ready_tasks, topological_order


def _tasks() -> list[Task]:
    return [
        Task(id="TASK-001", description="a", priority=TaskPriority.LOW),
        Task(id="TASK-002", description="b", depends_on=["TASK-001"]),
        Task(id="TASK-003", description="c", priority=TaskPriority.CRITICAL),
        Task(id="TASK-004", description="d", depends_on=["TASK-002", "TASK-003"]),
    ]


def test_topological_order_respects_dependencies():
    order = [t.id for t in topological_order(_tasks())]
    assert order.index("TASK-001") < order.index("TASK-002")
    assert order.index("TASK-002") < order.index("TASK-004")
    assert order.index("TASK-003") < order.index("TASK-004")
    assert sorted(order) == ["TASK-001", "TASK-002", "TASK-003", "TASK-004"]


def test_priority_breaks_ties_among_ready():
    order = [t.id for t in topological_order(_tasks())]
    # TASK-003 (critical) and TASK-001 (low) are both initially ready.
    assert order[0] == "TASK-003"
    assert order[1] == "TASK-001"


def test_unknown_dependency_rejected():
    tasks = [Task(id="TASK-001", description="x", depends_on=["TASK-999"])]
    with pytest.raises(TaskGraphError):
        topological_order(tasks)


def test_cycle_rejected():
    tasks = [
        Task(id="A", description="x", depends_on=["B"]),
        Task(id="B", description="y", depends_on=["A"]),
    ]
    with pytest.raises(TaskGraphError, match="cycle"):
        topological_order(tasks)


def test_duplicate_ids_rejected():
    tasks = [Task(id="A", description="x"), Task(id="A", description="y")]
    with pytest.raises(TaskGraphError):
        topological_order(tasks)


def test_ready_tasks():
    tasks = _tasks()
    tasks[0].status = TaskStatus.COMPLETED
    assert {t.id for t in ready_tasks(tasks)} == {"TASK-002", "TASK-003"}


def test_skipped_dependency_satisfies():
    tasks = _tasks()
    tasks[0].status = TaskStatus.SKIPPED
    tasks[1].status = TaskStatus.COMPLETED
    tasks[2].status = TaskStatus.COMPLETED
    assert {t.id for t in ready_tasks(tasks)} == {"TASK-004"}


def test_in_progress_not_ready():
    tasks = [Task(id="A", description="x")]
    tasks[0].status = TaskStatus.IN_PROGRESS
    assert ready_tasks(tasks) == []

from __future__ import annotations

from datetime import UTC, datetime

import pytest

from forgex.orchestration.events import EventRecorder

    ErrorRepository,
    EventRepository,
    ProjectRepository,
    RunRepository,
    TaskRepository,
)

EXPECTED_TABLES = {"projects", "runs", "tasks", "events", "errors"}


def _project(tmp_path) -> ProjectState:
    return ProjectState(
        project_id="p1",
        project_path=str(tmp_path / "proj"),
        status=WorkflowStatus.PLANNING,
    )


def test_migrations_create_tables(tmp_path):
    db = Database(tmp_path / "state.db")
    names = {
        r["name"] for r in db.conn.execute("SELECT name FROM sqlite_master WHERE type='table'")
    }
    assert EXPECTED_TABLES <= names
    db.close()


def test_migrations_idempotent(tmp_path):
    db = Database(tmp_path / "state.db")
    assert run_migrations(db.conn) == 1  # second call is a no-op
    db.close()


def test_project_upsert(tmp_path):
    db = Database(tmp_path / "state.db")
    repo = ProjectRepository(db)
    state = _project(tmp_path)
    repo.upsert(state)
    state.status = WorkflowStatus.BUILDING

from __future__ import annotations

from datetime import UTC, datetime

from forgex.orchestration.stores import open_stores
from forgex.schemas.error import ErrorRecord
from forgex.schemas.event import EventType
from forgex.schemas.project import ProjectState
from forgex.schemas.task import Task


def test_open_stores_creates_db_and_mirrors(tmp_path):
    fx = tmp_path / ".forgex"
    fx.mkdir()
    with open_stores(tmp_path) as stores:
        assert stores.db is not None
        state = ProjectState(project_id="p1", project_path=str(tmp_path))
        state.errors.append(
            ErrorRecord(ts=datetime.now(UTC), source="builder", kind="ToolError", message="boom")
        )
        stores.save_state(state)
        stores.save_tasks([Task(id="TASK-001", description="x")], project_id="p1")
        stores.recorder.record(EventType.RUN_STARTED, {"request": "demo"}, run_id="r1")

        assert stores.projects.get("p1") is not None
        assert [t.id for t in stores.task_repo.list("p1")] == ["TASK-001"]
        assert stores.events_repo.count() == 1
        assert len(stores.errors.list("p1")) == 1
    # durable JSON is the source of truth
    assert (fx / "state.json").is_file()
    assert (fx / "tasks.json").is_file()
    assert (fx / "events.jsonl").read_text(encoding="utf-8").count("\n") == 1


def test_open_stores_degraded_mode_never_creates_db(tmp_path):
    fx = tmp_path / ".forgex"
    fx.mkdir()
    with open_stores(tmp_path, create_db=False) as stores:
        assert stores.db is None
        assert stores.runs is None
        stores.recorder.record(EventType.PROJECT_INITIALIZED, {})
    assert not (fx / "state.db").exists()
    assert (fx / "events.jsonl").read_text(encoding="utf-8").strip() != ""

Thought Process
Phase 3 — the model layer. You didn't specify a provider, so I've shipped all three adapters (Groq wired as the default, generic OpenAI-compatible, local) — tell me at the end which one you'll test live and I'll target it in Phase 5.

PHASE 3 — PROVIDER SYSTEM
3.0 Design decisions (ADR additions)
Agents never see SDKs or HTTP. They call ModelRouter; the router resolves config bindings → factory → adapter. Providers are pure protocol implementations over injectable httpx clients (fully testable offline via MockTransport).
Retryable classification lives on the error taxonomy (exc.retryable): 429/5xx/timeouts/network → retried with exponential backoff; 401/403/4xx → never. ProviderError.attempts carries the attempt count for observability (§40).
Fallback (§30) is router-owned: after a provider exhausts retries (or fails auth — it's unusable), the router walks fallback.providers, using each provider's default_model (explicit, no guessing). Every fallback is logged.
Capability catalog is advisory, never authoritative (§65). Known models get metadata; unknown models get conservative defaults (no tools, no JSON, no streaming) and the router adapts requests accordingly (drops unsupported fields). Users extend/override via [models.capabilities."<model>"].
Secrets: config stores only env-var names. New ~/.forgex/.env + project .env loader (real env always wins; project file beats global). forgex config setup writes keys to ~/.forgex/.env with chmod 600. A redactor scrubs any accidental key leakage from provider error text (§46).
Usage tracking (§40–41): every router call records a model_usage row (migration v2) — real token counts only, None when unreported, never fabricated.
models/__init__ deliberately excludes router/factory to break an import cycle with forgex.config (documented in the module).

src/forgex/schemas/error.py ✏ — replace the ProviderError / AuthenticationError / RateLimitError block (everything else in the file stays):

python

class ProviderError(ForgexError):
    """Provider-level failure. `retryable` drives the retry policy (§29);
    `attempts` records how many attempts were made (§40)."""

    exit_code = 5
    default_retryable: bool = False
    attempts: int = 1

    def __init__(self, message: str, *, retryable: bool | None = None) -> None:
        super().__init__(message)
        self.retryable = self.default_retryable if retryable is None else retryable


class AuthenticationError(ProviderError):
    exit_code = 6
    default_retryable = False


class RateLimitError(ProviderError):
    exit_code = 7
    default_retryable = True
src/forgex/schemas/result.py ✚

python

"""Result schemas — model usage records (spec sections 40, 41).

Token counts are stored exactly as reported by the provider. When usage
is not reported, values stay None — never fabricated (spec section 41).
"""

from __future__ import annotations

from datetime import UTC, datetime
from pydantic import BaseModel, ConfigDict, Field


class ModelUsageRecord(BaseModel):
    model_config = ConfigDict(extra="forbid")

    ts: datetime = Field(default_factory=lambda: datetime.now(UTC))
    run_id: str | None = None
    agent: str
    provider: str
    model: str
    input_tokens: int | None = None
    output_tokens: int | None = None
    total_tokens: int | None = None
    latency_ms: float | None = None
    attempts: int = 1
    success: bool = True
    error: str | None = None

Retry engine
src/forgex/orchestration/retry.py ✚

python

"""Retry policy for transient failures (spec section 29).

Retried: rate limits, provider timeouts, transient network errors, 5xx.
Never retried: authentication failures, invalid requests, permission
errors. Exponential backoff with optional jitter; `sleep` and `rand` are
injectable so tests run instantly and deterministically.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import asyncio
import random
from collections.abc import Awaitable, Callable
from dataclasses import dataclass
from typing import TypeVar

T = TypeVar("T")


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class RetryPolicy:
    max_attempts: int = 3
    initial_delay: float = 1.0
    backoff_factor: float = 2.0
    max_delay: float = 30.0
    jitter: bool = True


def is_retryable(exc: BaseException) -> bool:
    return bool(getattr(exc, "retryable", False))


async def run_with_retries(
    func: Callable[[], Awaitable[T]],
    *,
    policy: RetryPolicy | None = None,
    retryable: Callable[[BaseException], bool] = is_retryable,
    sleep: Callable[[float], Awaitable[None]] = asyncio.sleep,
    rand: Callable[[], float] = random.random,
    on_retry: Callable[[int, BaseException, float], None] | None = None,
) -> tuple[T, int]:
    """Run `func` with retries. Returns ``(result, attempts)``; re-raises the
    last error when attempts are exhausted or the error is not retryable."""
    policy = policy or RetryPolicy()
    delay = policy.initial_delay
    attempts = 0
    while True:
        attempts += 1
        try:
            return await func(), attempts
        except Exception as exc:
            if attempts >= policy.max_attempts or not retryable(exc):
                raise
            wait = min(delay, policy.max_delay)
            if policy.jitter:
                wait = wait * (0.5 + 0.5 * rand())
            if on_retry is not None:
                on_retry(attempts, exc, wait)
            await sleep(wait)
            delay = min(delay * policy.backoff_factor, policy.max_delay)

"""Model capability metadata (spec section 31).

Never assume a model supports a feature: the router reads these and
adapts requests (drops tools / response_format when unsupported).
Unknown models get conservative defaults — honest "unknown", not guesses.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

from pydantic import BaseModel, ConfigDict


class ModelCapabilities(BaseModel):
    model_config = ConfigDict(extra="forbid")

    model: str
    context_window: int | None = None  # None = unknown
    supports_tools: bool = False
    supports_json: bool = False
    supports_streaming: bool = False

"""OpenAI-compatible chat-completions adapter.

Works with any server implementing POST /chat/completions and GET /models
(OpenAI, Groq, llama.cpp, Ollama, vLLM, LM Studio, ...). Maps HTTP
failures onto the FORGEX error taxonomy with retryable classification
(spec sections 13, 28, 29) and redacts any accidental secret leakage
from error text (spec section 46).
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import json
import logging
import time
from collections.abc import AsyncIterator
from typing import Any

import httpx

from forgex.models.base import (
    Message,
    ModelProvider,
    ModelRequest,
    ModelResponse,
    ModelUsage,
    ProviderHealth,
    ToolCall,
)
from forgex.orchestration.retry import RetryPolicy, run_with_retries
from forgex.schemas.error import AuthenticationError, ProviderError, RateLimitError
from forgex.utils.redact import redact

logger = logging.getLogger("forgex.models")


def parse_sse_content(line: str) -> str | None:
    """Parse one SSE line: content delta, '' for [DONE], None to skip."""
    if not line.startswith("data:"):
        return None
    data = line[len("data:"):].strip()
    if not data:
        return None
    if data == "[DONE]":
        return ""
    try:
        chunk = json.loads(data)
    except ValueError:
        return None
    choices = chunk.get("choices") or []
    if not choices:
        return None
    content = (choices[0].get("delta") or {}).get("content")
    return content if content else None


class OpenAICompatibleProvider(ModelProvider):
    """Adapter for OpenAI-compatible HTTP APIs."""

    def __init__(
        self,
        name: str,
        *,
        base_url: str,
        api_key: str | None = None,
        timeout_seconds: float = 60.0,
        max_retries: int = 2,
        http_client: httpx.AsyncClient | None = None,

src/forgex/models/factory.py ✚

python

"""Provider factory: config → provider instance.

API keys are resolved from the environment (after .env loading) — secrets
never live in configuration files (spec sections 26, 46).
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import os

from forgex.config.schema import ForgexConfig, ProviderConfig
from forgex.models.base import ModelProvider
from forgex.models.groq import DEFAULT_API_KEY_ENV as GROQ_KEY_ENV
from forgex.models.groq import DEFAULT_BASE_URL as GROQ_BASE_URL
from forgex.models.groq import GroqProvider
from forgex.models.local import LocalProvider
from forgex.models.openai_compatible import OpenAICompatibleProvider
from forgex.schemas.error import ConfigError


def resolve_api_key(config: ProviderConfig) -> str | None:
    env_name = config.api_key_env
    if env_name is None and config.type == "groq":
        env_name = GROQ_KEY_ENV
    if not env_name:
        return None
    return os.environ.get(env_name) or None


def build_provider(
    name: str,
    config: ProviderConfig,
    *,
    http_client=None,
) -> ModelProvider:
    """Build the provider instance for a configured provider entry."""
    kwargs: dict = {
        "api_key": resolve_api_key(config),
        "timeout_seconds": config.timeout_seconds,
        "max_retries": config.max_retries,
    }
    if http_client is not None:
        kwargs["http_client"] = http_client

    if config.type == "groq":
        return GroqProvider(base_url=config.base_url or GROQ_BASE_URL, **kwargs)
    if config.type == "local":
        if not config.base_url:
            msg = (
                f"Provider '{name}' (local) needs a base_url, "
                "e.g. http://localhost:11434/v1"
            )
            raise ConfigError(msg)
        return LocalProvider(base_url=config.base_url, **kwargs)
    if not config.base_url:
        msg = f"Provider '{name}' (openai_compatible) needs a base_url"
        raise ConfigError(msg)
    return OpenAICompatibleProvider(name, base_url=config.base_url, **kwargs)


def provider_names(config: ForgexConfig) -> list[str]:
    return sorted(config.providers)

src/forgex/config/loader.py ✏ — add import os at the top, then append these functions (everything else unchanged):

python

ENV_FILE_NAME = ".env"


def parse_env_file(path: Path) -> dict[str, str]:
    """Minimal .env parser: comments, optional 'export ', KEY=VALUE, quotes."""
    values: dict[str, str] = {}
    for line in path.read_text(encoding="utf-8").splitlines():
        line = line.strip()
        if not line or line.startswith("#"):
            continue
        if line.startswith("export "):
            line = line[7:]
        if "=" not in line:
            continue
        key, _, value = line.partition("=")
        key, value = key.strip(), value.strip()
        if len(value) >= 2 and value[0] == value[-1] and value[0] in ("'", '"'):
            value = value[1:-1]
        if key:
            values[key] = value
    return values


def load_env_files(project_root: Path | None = None) -> list[Path]:
    """Load ~/.forgex/.env then <project>/.env into os.environ with setdefault.

    Priority: real environment always wins; project .env beats global .env
    (more specific context, mirroring the config file layering).
    """
    paths = [Path.home() / FORGEX_DIR_NAME / ENV_FILE_NAME]
    if project_root is not None:
        paths.append(Path(project_root) / ENV_FILE_NAME)
    loaded: list[Path] = []
    for path in paths:
        if not path.is_file():
            continue
        try:
            values = parse_env_file(path)
        except OSError:
            continue
        for key, value in values.items():
            os.environ.setdefault(key, value)
        loaded.append(path)
    return loaded


def store_secret(env_var: str, value: str, *, global_dir: Path | None = None) -> Path:
    """Persist a secret to ~/.forgex/.env (chmod 600). Never into config files."""
    target = (global_dir or Path.home() / FORGEX_DIR_NAME) / ENV_FILE_NAME
    target.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    existing: dict[str, str] = parse_env_file(target) if target.is_file() else {}
    existing[env_var] = value
    lines = [f"{key}={val}" for key, val in existing.items()]
    target.write_text("\n".join(lines) + "\n", encoding="utf-8")
    os.chmod(target, 0o600)
    return target
src/forgex/utils/redact.py ✚

python

"""Secret redaction (spec section 46).

Secrets must never leak into logs, events, errors, or the database. Any
code formatting external text (provider error bodies, later: command
output) passes through redact() with the known secrets.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

from collections.abc import Iterable

MASK = "***REDACTED***"


def redact(text: str, secrets: Iterable[str | None]) -> str:
    """Replace every occurrence of each secret with the mask."""
    result = text
    for secret in secrets:
        # Ignore trivially short values — redacting "abc" mangles normal text.
        if secret and len(secret) >= 4:
            result = result.replace(secret, MASK)
    return result
