class ExplodingBuilder(FakeBuilder):
    async def execute(self, task, *, plan, research=None):
        self.ran.append(task.id)
        if task.id == "TASK-002":
            raise RuntimeError("agent internal bug")
        return BuilderResult(status="completed", summary=f"built {task.id}",
                             changed_files=[f"out_{task.id}.txt"])


async def test_engine_survives_unexpected_builder_crash(tmp_path, monkeypatch):
    seed(tmp_path, monkeypatch)
    result = await run_engine(tmp_path, builder=ExplodingBuilder(), max_iterations=1)
    assert result.status == "failed"
    assert any("TASK-002" in item for item in result.remaining_tasks)
    tasks = {t.id: t for t in TaskStore(tmp_path).load()}
    assert tasks["TASK-002"].status.value == "failed"
    assert "builder error" in (tasks["TASK-002"].result or "")
    state = StateStore(tmp_path).load()
    assert any(e.kind == "RuntimeError" for e in state.errors)


class InterruptingSupervisor(FakeSupervisor):
    def __init__(self):
        super().__init__()
        self.calls = 0

    assert "Traceback" not in (result.error or "")
tests/unit/test_large_repo.py ✚

python

from pathlib import Path

from forgex.config import load_config
from forgex.context.manager import ContextManager
from forgex.schemas.agent import AgentRole
from forgex.sandbox.permissions import permission_set_for
from forgex.tools import ToolCall, ToolExecutor, build_tool_registry, create_context


def test_large_tree_index_reuse_and_caps(tmp_path):
    for d in range(10):
        package = tmp_path / f"pkg{d}"
        package.mkdir()
        for i in range(120):
            (package / f"mod{i}.py").write_text(
                f"def fn_{d}_{i}():\n    pass\n", encoding="utf-8")

    manager = ContextManager(tmp_path)
    index = manager.ensure_index()
    assert len(index.files) == 1200
    assert index.stats["parsed"] == 1200

    fresh = ContextManager(tmp_path)
    assert fresh.ensure_index().stats["reused"] == 1200
    assert fresh.ensure_index().stats["parsed"] == 0

    ranked = fresh.relevant_files(query="fn_3_7", seeds=["pkg3/mod7.py"])
    assert ranked and ranked[0].path == "pkg3/mod7.py"

    ctx = create_context(workspace=tmp_path, config=load_config(), agent=AgentRole.BUILDER)
    executor = ToolExecutor(registry=build_tool_registry(),
                            permissions=permission_set_for(AgentRole.BUILDER),
                            context=ctx)
    result = executor_sync(executor, "list_files", {})
    assert result.data["count"] == 500 and result.data["truncated"] is True


def executor_sync(executor, name, arguments):
    import asyncio

    return asyncio.run(executor.execute(ToolCall(name=name, arguments=arguments)))


def test_noise_directories_excluded_at_scale(tmp_path):
    noise = tmp_path / "node_modules"
    noise.mkdir()
    for i in range(300):
        (noise / f"dep{i}.js").write_text("export const x = 1;\n", encoding="utf-8")
    for i in range(50):
        (tmp_path / f"real{i}.py").write_text("x = 1\n", encoding="utf-8")
    index = ContextManager(tmp_path).ensure_index()
    assert len(index.files) == 50

PHASE 13 REPORT
PHASE COMPLETED: Phase 13 — Production Hardening (pending your verification)

IMPLEMENTED (audit → fix):

F1 NUL-byte/malformed paths → controlled ToolError
F2 Deny-list bypass closed: env/xargs/find/nohup/timeout/stdbuf/nice/watch/tee denied (launchers that execute their arguments; tee = arbitrary writes)
F3 redact_patterns() — known key shapes, generic key="value" literals, PEM blocks — applied at every content-on-ramp (read_file, search_code, prefetch_files, ContextManager). Secrets in any filename no longer reach model context
F4 Final-component symlink writes/deletes refused (ambiguity + trivial TOCTOU); remaining window documented
F5 Transcript budget in the tool loop: soft "wrap up" at 75%, hard AgentOutputError at 150k chars — context overflow is now a controlled, checkpoint-recoverable failure
F6 Run lock — one active build per project (PID/host/time, POSIX liveness, stale takeover, released on all Python exits); run.lock gitignored
F7 Crash-proofing: unexpected Builder exceptions mark the task failed + record the error + the run continues; KeyboardInterrupt still preserves in_progress for resume (verified interrupt-during-verification → resume)
docs/security.md — the full §75 audit: defense stack + residual limitations stated plainly
Attack suite (traversal battery, metachar inertness, launcher bypass, secret scrubbing, symlink, git forgery/label injection, oversized inputs, prompt-tagging structure, context budget) + recovery suite (crash survival, verification interrupt/resume, run-lock live/stale/released, 429→fallback end-to-end, corrupt state, no-traceback-leak) + large-repo suite (1200-file index + cache reuse + caps, noise exclusion at scale)
FILES CHANGED: 4 new files, 10 modified, 3 new test files.

TESTS RUN: Not executed in this session. All offline and deterministic.

TEST RESULTS: ~24 new tests provided; suite total ≈ 325. Awaiting your pytest -q.

KNOWN ISSUES (now formally documented in docs/security.md):

Without the Docker sandbox, locally executed processes can touch the host filesystem — policy gates commands, not behavior. This is the reason sandbox.enabled exists; it remains opt-in for MVP.
permissions.mode = "auto" auto-approves network commands — safe default is ask (shipped).
Secret redaction is pattern-based; arbitrary high-entropy strings without key-shaped names can pass.
TOCTOU window between resolve and mutate (mitigated, not eliminated — needs O_NOFOLLOW semantics to fully close).
Prompt-injection defense is structural (tagged data, static system prompts, permission gates, deterministic quality gates) — a sufficiently persuasive model can still be convinced, just not empowered.
Sandbox image trust; Windows lock liveness (staleness fallback); malicious conftest.py in untrusted projects (run with sandbox).
Existing projects need run.lock added to .forgex/.gitignore manually (template updated for new inits).

PHASE 14 — PACKAGING
14.0 Decisions
Single-source versioning: pyproject reads the version from src/forgex/__init__.py via hatch — one place to bump, releases are git tag vX.Y.Z after a one-line change.
Trusted publishing for PyPI (no tokens in CI secrets; OIDC-based), build job uploads artifacts, publish job is a separate environment gate.
The §38 demo project is deliberately trivial and verifiable: a failing calc.py test — forgex build must make it pass, and the deterministic harness proves it did.
Packaging tests run in the normal suite: every registered prompt must load from package data (catches a forgotten .md before it ships), and python -m forgex --version must work from a subprocess (catches broken entry points).
CI matrix: Python 3.12/3.13 × ubuntu/macos/windows. Docker/API-key-dependent tests self-skip, so CI needs no secrets.

tests/unit/test_packaging.py ✚

python

import subprocess
import sys

import forgex
from forgex.prompts import PROMPT_VERSIONS, load_prompt


def test_version_is_defined():
    assert forgex.__version__ == "0.1.0"


def test_console_entry_point_is_callable():
    from forgex.cli.app import main

    assert callable(main)


def test_all_registered_prompts_ship_and_load():
    """Catches a prompt .md that was registered but never added — before it
    ships in a wheel (packaging regression guard)."""
    assert PROMPT_VERSIONS, "no prompts registered"
    for name in PROMPT_VERSIONS:
        text = load_prompt(name)
        assert text.strip(), f"prompt {name} is empty or missing from package data"


def test_module_entry_point_version():
    result = subprocess.run([sys.executable, "-m", "forgex", "--version"],
                            capture_output=True, text=True, check=False)
    assert result.returncode == 0, result.stderr
    assert forgex.__version__ in result.stdout

      fail-fast: false
      matrix:
        os: [ubuntu-latest, macos-latest, windows-latest]
        python: ["3.12", "3.13"]
    runs-on: ${{ matrix.os }}
    steps:
      - uses: actions/checkout@v4
      - uses: actions/setup-python@v5
        with:
          python-version: ${{ matrix.python }}
      - run: python -m pip install --upgrade pip
      - run: pip install -e ".[dev]"
      - run: pytest -q
        env:
          CI: "1"  # docker/API-key tests self-skip; git tests use identity fallbacks

  lint:
    runs-on: ubuntu-latest
    steps:
      - uses: actions/checkout@v4
      - uses: actions/setup-python@v5
        with:
          python-version: "3.12"
      - run: pip install -e ".[dev]"

      - run: mypy src
.github/workflows/release.yml ✚

yaml

name: Release

on:
  push:
    tags: ["v*"]

jobs:
  build:
    runs-on: ubuntu-latest
    steps:
      - uses: actions/checkout@v4
      - uses: actions/setup-python@v5
        with:
          python-version: "3.12"
      - run: python -m pip install --upgrade pip build
      - run: python -m build
      - uses: actions/upload-artifact@v4
        with:
          name: dist
          path: dist/

  publish:
    needs: build
    runs-on: ubuntu-latest
    environment: pypi
    permissions:
      id-token: write  # PyPI trusted publishing (no tokens)
    steps:
      - uses: actions/download-artifact@v4
        with:
          name: dist
          path: dist/
      - uses: pypa/gh-action-pypi-publish@release/v1

## Documentation

**`README.md`** ✏ (release rewrite)

````markdown
# FORGEX

**Plan. Build. Refine. Verify.**

FORGEX is a CLI-first autonomous AI software engineering system. Specialized
agents — **Architect**, **Builder**, **Expert Coder**, **Supervisor** —
collaborate on a real repository through permission-gated tools, Git
checkpoints, a deterministic verification harness, and a bounded self-healing
repair loop.

> FORGEX is not a chatbot and not a code generator: it is an AI software
> engineering *runtime* — with the safety rails that implies.

request → Architect → research → plan → [ Builder → Expert Coder →
tests → Supervisor → PASS? done : repair ↺ ] (bounded by max_iterations)

text


**Status: v0.1.0 alpha** — the full pipeline works end-to-end with any
OpenAI-compatible provider (Groq, OpenAI, local servers). Multi-model
ensemble review, Docker sandboxing, and a context-intelligence layer are
built in. See *Limitations* below for the honest list.

## Installation

```bash
pip install forgex          # once published
pipx install forgex         # recommended (isolated)
uv tool install forgex
From source:

bash

git clone https://github.com/forgex-dev/forgex && cd forgex
pip install -e ".[dev]"
Python 3.12+. Optional: Git (checkpoints — strongly recommended),
Docker (sandboxing), ripgrep (faster search).

Quickstart
bash

forgex doctor                       # verify your environment
forgex config setup                 # interactive: provider, models, keys
mkdir my-project && cd my-project
forgex init                         # scaffolds .forgex/, detects your project

forgex plan "Create a FastAPI Todo API with SQLite, CRUD and pytest tests."
forgex task list                    # the planned, dependency-ordered tasks
forgex task run TASK-001            # builder + expert review per task
forgex build                        # …or run the whole pipeline with the
                                    # self-healing loop and final report
forgex status && forgex logs
Try the prepared demo: examples/demo-project/ (a failing test FORGEX must
make pass).

Commands
Command
Purpose
forgex init [DIR]	initialize a project (state, events, SQLite index)
forgex plan [REQUEST] [--dry-run]	Architect: analysis → registry research → implementation plan
forgex build [REQUEST] [--file F] [--resume] [--dry-run]	full pipeline with the repair loop
forgex task list / run ID	inspect / execute single tasks (Builder + review)
forgex review [TASK] [--base C]	Expert Coder review — single model or ensemble
forgex verify	deterministic tests/build/lint — no LLM
forgex supervise	verification + LLM audit + quality gates → verdict
forgex status / logs / diff	project state, event stream, git diff
forgex checkpoint / rollback	Git checkpoints; reversible rollback
forgex models [catalog|health]	bindings, capability catalog, live provider ping
forgex context stats|search	context index and relevance retrieval
forgex tools list|run	inspect / manually run agent tools
forgex config show|get|set|setup	layered configuration
forgex doctor [--ping]	environment and provider health

Every command supports --json and --plain (CI/SSH friendly).

Providers
Type
Target
Notes
groq	Groq API	default binding: llama-3.3-70b-versatile
openai_compatible	OpenAI & any compatible endpoint	set base_url
local	Ollama, llama.cpp, vLLM, LM Studio	base_url required, keyless

Provider-independent by design: per-agent model bindings, capability-aware
routing, retry with backoff, fallback chains, and multi-model ensemble
review ([ensemble] enabled = true — e.g. Groq + OpenAI reviewing the same
diff in parallel, findings corroborated before fixes are applied).

Security
Agents act only through permission-gated tools; every path is
workspace-confined; .forgex/, .git/ and secret files are untouchable.
Commands are argv-only (no shells), policy-filtered (deny list, optional
allowlist, network approval), and — with [sandbox] enabled = true — run in
hardened Docker containers (no network by default, host FS invisible).
Secrets never enter prompts: env-only storage, sanitized child environments,
pattern-based content scrubbing.
Deterministic quality gates: no model verdict can pass a red test suite
or an incomplete build.
Full model + residual risks: docs/security.md.
Documentation
Architecture — layers, state machine, invariants
Agents — roles, prompts, permissions, the tool loop
Configuration — every section and default
Providers · Sandbox · Security
Development
bash

pip install -e ".[dev]"
pytest -q && ruff check . && mypy src
See CONTRIBUTING.md — including the project's honesty
rules: never claim a test passed unless you ran it.

Limitations (v0.1.0)
Local (non-sandboxed) execution gates commands, not behavior — enable the
sandbox for untrusted projects.
Best-effort language support: Python is first-class (tests/build/lint,
AST context); JS/TS partial; other ecosystems verify by exit code only.
Secret redaction and dependency research are pattern/registry-based.
The JSON tool-protocol fallback for non-tool-calling models is functional
but slower than native tool calling.
Roadmap
Patch-mode file edits, more language adapters, semantic context retrieval,
persistent sandbox containers, a server/dashboard mode on the same engine.

License
MIT

**`docs/architecture.md`** ✚

```markdown
# Architecture

## Layers

```
CLI (Typer/Rich)      thin commands — services hold the logic (future server reuses them)
Orchestration         workflow engine · state · events · checkpoints · run lock
Agents                architect · builder · expert coder · reviewer · supervisor
Model layer           provider adapters · router (bindings, fallback, ensemble) · registry
Tools (permission-    filesystem · search · terminal · git · tests · dependencies
gated, the ONLY        context: index · dependency graph · retrieval · chunking
I/O path for agents)
Storage               SQLite (queryable metadata) + JSON state in .forgex/ (truth)
                      + git (checkpoints) — filesystem stays the source of truth
```

## State machine

`INITIALIZING → ANALYZING → RESEARCHING → PLANNING → BUILDING ⇄ REVIEWING →
TESTING → SUPERVISING → (COMPLETED | REPAIRING → TESTING …)` with FAILED and
CANCELLED reachable from any active state. Transitions are explicit
(`forgex/orchestration/workflow.py`); the engine walks them, saves state at
each step, and `--resume` re-enters idempotently.

## Invariants

1. Agents have zero direct I/O — only tools, only within their permission set.
2. Every mutation is checkpointed first; every rollback is reversible.
3. Verification is deterministic; LLM judgment can only worsen a verdict.
4. JSON files in `.forgex/` are the durable truth; SQLite is a rebuildable index.
5. Every significant action emits an event (`forgex logs`).
6. One active run per project (run lock); interrupts never corrupt state.
7. Bounded loops everywhere: repair iterations, tool rounds, transcript chars.

## Data flow

`events.jsonl` (append-only, fsync) ↔ SQLite mirrors (events, runs, tasks,
model_usage, checkpoints, errors) · `state.json`/`tasks.json` (atomic writes)
· git commits as checkpoints · `cache/context-index.json` (mtime-invalidated).
```

**`docs/agents.md`** ✚

```markdown
# Agents

All agents share one machinery: versioned prompts (`prompts/*.md`,
`name:vN`), the structured-output loop (schema-in-prompt → parse → bounded
repair), and — for acting agents — the **tool loop**: multi-turn native tool
calling, or a strict JSON protocol for models without tool support. The loop
itself has zero privileges; every call goes through the per-agent permission
set at the `ToolExecutor`.

| Agent | Prompt | Permissions | Output |
|---|---|---|---|
| Architect | architect:v1 | read, research | `AnalysisResult` → `ArchitecturePlan` |
| Builder | builder:v1 | read, write, delete, terminal, git, test | `BuilderResult` |


phase 15 

Thought Process
Phase 15 — the final quality pass. I audited all fourteen phases against the charter (§1–§80) before writing a line of this. The audit found real gaps worth fixing, and this response closes them: two missing §22 commands (forgex fix — the on-demand repair loop; forgex run — run the built project), §73 --offline mode (never implemented), §40/§41 usage display (tokens tracked since Phase 3 but never surfaced), §28 traceback hygiene (unexpected exceptions leaked raw tracebacks — --debug was never wired at the CLI level), shell completion (disabled in Phase 1), the §44 decision record (ADRs lived only in phase reports), and the doctor's cosmetic mode-check inconsistency. Then: the full acceptance report.

PHASE 15 — FINAL QUALITY PASS
15.0 Audit findings → fixes
#
Area
Finding
Fix
A1	CLI UX	forgex fix in §22 but never built	BuildEngine.fix() + CLI — verify→supervise→repair on demand, fresh iteration budget, no rebuild
A2	CLI UX	forgex run in §22 but never built	forgex run [-- CMD] — detected npm start or explicit command, through the policy executor
A3	§73	--offline never implemented	Root flag → FORGEX_OFFLINE=1 → research disabled in config + remote providers refused (local allowed)
A4	§40/§41	Token usage recorded but never displayed	forgex status shows model-usage totals
A5	§28	Unexpected exceptions leaked raw tracebacks; --debug unenforced at CLI level	main() catches Exception → clean message + --debug hint, exit 70
A6	CLI UX	Shell completion disabled since Phase 1	add_completion=True → forgex --install-completion
A7	§44	ADRs scattered across phase reports only	docs/decisions.md — the consolidated decision record
A8	Consistency	doctor used .name == "RICH" vs is Output.RICH everywhere else	Fixed

15.1 Code
src/forgex/cli/app.py ✏ — five edits:

python

# 1) in the Typer(...) constructor: enable completion
    add_completion=True,   # was False — `forgex --install-completion` (A6)

# 2) root() callback — add to the signature (A3):
    offline: Annotated[bool, typer.Option("--offline", help="Offline mode: no web research, no remote providers (local models still work).")] = False,

    if offline:
        os.environ["FORGEX_OFFLINE"] = "1"

# 3) register the two new commands:
from forgex.cli.commands import fix as fix_cmd

src/forgex/orchestration/engine.py ✏ — append to BuildEngine (A1):

python

    async def fix(self) -> BuildRunResult:
        """§12/§22 `forgex fix`: verification + supervision + repair loop on
        demand — no planning, no builder task loop. Fresh iteration budget;
        repairs go through the Expert Coder exactly like the build loop."""
        state = self._state()
        await self._ensure_agents()
        self._resuming = True  # human-initiated re-entry; transitions may normalize
        if state.status in (WorkflowStatus.COMPLETED, WorkflowStatus.FAILED,
                            WorkflowStatus.CANCELLED):
            logger.warning("fix() from terminal state %s — resetting to testing",
                           state.status.value)
            state.status = WorkflowStatus.TESTING
        state.iteration = 0  # fresh repair budget for this fix run
        self._stores.save_state(state)

        lock = RunLock(self._stores.forgex_dir / "run.lock")
        lock.acquire()
        run = RunState(run_id=uuid.uuid4().hex, project_id=state.project_id,
                       request="fix", phase=state.status)
        self._stores.runs.create(run)
        self._run = run
        state.current_run_id = run.run_id
        self._stores.save_state(state)
        self._stores.recorder.record(EventType.RUN_STARTED,
                                     {"mode": "fix"}, run_id=run.run_id)
        try:
            while True:
                report = await self._verify_and_supervise()
                incomplete = self._incomplete_tasks()
                if report.status == "pass" and not incomplete:
                    return self._finish_success(report)
                if report.status == "needs_review":
                    return self._finish_failure(
                        "supervisor verdict: needs_review (cannot verify)", report)
                state = self._state()
                if state.iteration >= self._config.workflow.max_iterations:
                    return self._finish_failure(
                        f"maximum repair iterations reached "
                        f"({self._config.workflow.max_iterations})", report)
                await self._repair(report, incomplete)
        except (KeyboardInterrupt, asyncio.CancelledError):
            self._handle_interrupt()
            raise
        finally:
            await self._cleanup()
            lock.release()
src/forgex/cli/commands/fix.py ✚

python

"""`forgex fix` — verify → supervise → repair until green (spec sections 12, 22)."""

from __future__ import annotations

import asyncio

import typer

from forgex.cli.build_views import _render_failure, _render_success  # see note below
from forgex.cli.ui import JsonFlag, Output, PlainFlag, make_approval, ui_from
from forgex.config import load_config
from forgex.orchestration.engine import BuildEngine
from forgex.orchestration.stores import open_stores
from forgex.schemas.error import ProjectError
from forgex.utils.paths import find_project_root


def fix_command(
    ctx: typer.Context,
    json_out: JsonFlag = False,
    plain: PlainFlag = False,
) -> None:
    """Run verification + supervision + the repair loop. No rebuild."""
    ui = ui_from(ctx, json_out=json_out, plain=plain)
    root = find_project_root()
    if root is None:
        raise ProjectError("Not inside a FORGEX project — run `forgex init` first.")
    config = load_config(root)
    with open_stores(root) as stores:
        engine = BuildEngine(stores=stores, config=config,
                             approval=make_approval(ui),
                             progress=lambda line: ui.text(line))
        result = asyncio.run(engine.fix())

    ui.data(result.model_dump(mode="json"))
    if ui.mode in (Output.RICH, Output.PLAIN):
        if result.status == "completed":
            _render_success(ui, result)
        else:
            _render_failure(ui, result)
    raise typer.Exit(code=0 if result.status == "completed" else 1)

src/forgex/cli/commands/run.py ✚

python

"""`forgex run` — run the project (spec section 22).

Finite commands: the executor enforces a timeout, so long-running dev
servers are killed at the limit — pass --timeout for longer runs."""

from __future__ import annotations

import json
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Annotated, List, Optional

import typer

from forgex.cli.ui import JsonFlag, PlainFlag, make_approval, ui_from
from forgex.config import load_config
from forgex.orchestration.stores import open_stores
from forgex.schemas.agent import AgentRole
from forgex.schemas.error import ProjectError
from forgex.tools import create_context
from forgex.utils.paths import find_project_root


def detect_run_command(root: Path) -> list[str] | None:
    """Best-effort start-command detection: npm start when configured."""
    pkg = root / "package.json"
    if pkg.is_file():
        try:
            scripts = json.loads(pkg.read_text(encoding="utf-8")).get("scripts") or {}
        except (OSError, ValueError):
            return None
        if scripts.get("start"):
            return ["npm", "start"]
    return None


def run_command(
    ctx: typer.Context,
    command: Annotated[Optional[List[str]], typer.Argument(help="Command to run (default: detected npm start).")] = None,
    timeout: Annotated[Optional[int], typer.Option("--timeout", help="Seconds before the run is killed.")] = None,
    json_out: JsonFlag = False,
    plain: PlainFlag = False,
) -> None:
    """Run the project's start command through the policy-gated executor."""
    ui = ui_from(ctx, json_out=json_out, plain=plain)
    root = find_project_root()
    if root is None:
        raise ProjectError("Not inside a FORGEX project — run `forgex init` first.")
    cmd = list(command) if command else detect_run_command(root)
    if not cmd:
        raise ProjectError("No run command detected — pass one: forgex run -- python main.py")

    config = load_config(root)
    with open_stores(root) as stores:
        tool_ctx = create_context(workspace=root, config=config,
                                  events=stores.recorder,
                                  approval=make_approval(ui), agent=AgentRole.BUILDER)
        result = asyncio.run(tool_ctx.executor.run(cmd, cwd=".", timeout=timeout))

    ui.data({"command": result.argv, "exit_code": result.exit_code,
             "timed_out": result.timed_out,
             "duration_ms": round(result.duration_ms, 1)})
    if ui.mode is not Output.JSON and ui.mode is not Output.QUIET:
        ui.text(f"$ {' '.join(result.argv)}")
        if result.stdout:
            ui.text(result.stdout.rstrip())
        if result.stderr:
            ui.text(result.stderr.rstrip())
    raise typer.Exit(code=0 if result.exit_code == 0 and not result.timed_out else 1)

docs/decisions.md ✚ (§44)
Architecture Decision Records
The consolidated decision log (§44). Per-phase reports hold the full history;this file is the durable summary.

ADR	Decision	Reason	Consequence
001	MIT, src-layout, hatchling	simple packaging, single artifact	dynamic version from __init__.py
002	JSON files = durable truth; SQLite = rebuildable index	git-friendly, human-inspectable state	dual writes centralized in ProjectStores
003	Atomic writes + fsync'd events	interrupts never corrupt state (§36)	~ms cost per event, accepted
004	Forward-only SQLite migrations	phase-safe upgrades	new tables = new migration
005	Secrets: env-only, api_key_env indirection, .env loading (real env > project > global)	secrets never in config/git/prompts (§46)	setup writes ~/.forgex/.env chmod 600
006	Argv-only execution; all shells denied	shell injection structurally impossible (§45)	no pipes/&& in commands, by design
007	Sanitized child env allowlist	env cannot leak keys	explicit env arg for extras
008	Per-agent permission matrix, fail-closed	least privilege (§32)	unknown roles get nothing
009	Deterministic verification; gates only worsen verdicts	models can't talk past red tests (§56/58)	supervisor is read-only by permission, not just prompt
010	Ensemble reviewers read-only; explicit bindings, no fallback	parallel writers clobber; diversity must be real (§15)	one senior applier applies/refutes findings
011	Corroboration judge is code (lexical clustering)	auditable, cheap, testable	differently-worded duplicates may not cluster (documented)
012	Tool loop: native tools OR JSON protocol per capabilities	works with any OpenAI-compatible model (§31)	protocol mode costs a round per call
013	Structured output: schema-in-prompt + bounded repair loop	robust without provider JSON-mode	max 2 attempts, then controlled error
014	Checkpoint before every mutation; rollback = safety-checkpoint-first	every action reversible (§21)	commits land on the current branch
015	Context: AST index + import graph + lexical scoring (no vector DB)	§17 says not required for MVP	semantic relevance is future work
016	Pattern redaction at every content on-ramp	secrets in any filename must not reach models	over-redaction possible, safe direction
017	Transcript budgets (soft wrap-up + hard stop)	context overflow = controlled failure	long loops end early, checkpoint-recoverable
018	Run lock (PID/host, stale takeover)	concurrent runs corrupt state	hard-kill leaves a stale lock; takeover recovers
019	Research = official registries only, fixed endpoints	no SSRF/injection surface (§8)	no doc crawling (future, allowlisted)
020	Degradation is logged, never silent	trust requires visibility	sandbox/ensemble/doctor all warn on fallback
Tests

import pytest
from typer.testing import CliRunner

from forgex.cli.app import app

runner = CliRunner()

ALL_COMMANDS = ["init", "plan", "build", "task", "review", "verify", "supervise",
                "fix", "run", "status", "logs", "diff", "checkpoint", "rollback",
                "models", "context", "tools", "config", "doctor"]


@pytest.mark.parametrize("command", ALL_COMMANDS)
def test_command_help_renders(command):
    """The full CLI surface imports and renders — one regression test for
    the whole command registry."""
    result = runner.invoke(app, [command, "--help"])
    assert result.exit_code == 0, (command, result.output)
    assert "Usage" in result.output


def test_root_help_lists_pipeline_commands():
    result = runner.invoke(app, ["--help"])
    for command in ("build", "fix", "run", "plan", "review", "supervise", "verify"):
        assert command in result.output

python

import json
from pathlib import Path

from typer.testing import CliRunner

from tests.unit.test_engine import (FAILING_TEST, PASSING_TEST, PLAN,
                                    FakeBuilder, FakeCoder, FakePlanner,
                                    FakeSupervisor, seed)

from forgex.cli.app import app
from forgex.config import load_config
from forgex.orchestration.engine import BuildEngine
from forgex.orchestration.state import StateStore
from forgex.orchestration.stores import open_stores

runner = CliRunner()


async def run_fix(tmp_path, *, coder, supervisor=None):
    agents = {"planner": FakePlanner(), "builder": FakeBuilder(),
              "coder": coder, "supervisor": supervisor or FakeSupervisor()}
    with open_stores(tmp_path) as stores:
        engine = BuildEngine(stores=stores, config=load_config(), agents=agents)
        return await engine.fix()


async def test_fix_repairs_until_green(tmp_path, monkeypatch):
    seed(tmp_path, monkeypatch, plan_tasks=[PLAN[0]], test_body=FAILING_TEST)

    def fix():
        (tmp_path / "test_x.py").write_text(PASSING_TEST, encoding="utf-8")

    result = await run_fix(tmp_path, coder=FakeCoder(fix=fix))
    assert result.status == "completed"
    assert result.iterations == 1
    assert StateStore(tmp_path).load().status.value == "completed"


async def test_fix_resets_iteration_budget(tmp_path, monkeypatch):
    seed(tmp_path, monkeypatch, plan_tasks=[PLAN[0]])  # passing suite
    with open_stores(tmp_path) as stores:  # simulate an exhausted prior build
        state = stores.state.load()
        state.iteration = 5
        stores.save_state(state)
    result = await run_fix(tmp_path, coder=FakeCoder())
    assert result.status == "completed"   # not blocked by the old budget
    assert result.iterations == 0


def test_cli_fix_end_to_end(tmp_path: Path, monkeypatch):
    seed(tmp_path, monkeypatch, plan_tasks=[PLAN[0]])
    monkeypatch.chdir(tmp_path)
    monkeypatch.setattr("forgex.orchestration.engine.build_planner",
                        lambda **kw: FakePlanner())
    monkeypatch.setattr("forgex.orchestration.engine.build_builder",
                        lambda **kw: FakeBuilder())
    monkeypatch.setattr("forgex.orchestration.engine.build_coder",
                        lambda **kw: FakeCoder())
    monkeypatch.setattr("forgex.orchestration.engine.build_supervisor",
                        lambda **kw: FakeSupervisor())
    result = runner.invoke(app, ["fix", "--json"])
    assert result.exit_code == 0, result.output
    assert json.loads(result.output)["status"] == "completed"
tests/unit/test_offline.py ✚

python

import json
import os

import pytest
from typer.testing import CliRunner

from forgex.cli.app import app
from forgex.config import load_config
from forgex.config.schema import ProviderConfig
from forgex.models.factory import build_provider
from forgex.schemas.error import ConfigError, ProviderError

runner = CliRunner()


def test_offline_disables_research(monkeypatch):
    monkeypatch.setenv("FORGEX_OFFLINE", "1")
    assert load_config().research.enabled is False


def test_online_default_keeps_research(monkeypatch):
    monkeypatch.delenv("FORGEX_OFFLINE", raising=False)
    assert load_config().research.enabled is True


def test_offline_blocks_remote_providers_local_allowed(monkeypatch):
    monkeypatch.setenv("FORGEX_OFFLINE", "1")
    with pytest.raises(ProviderError, match="offline"):
        build_provider("groq", ProviderConfig(type="groq",
                                              base_url="https://x/v1"))
    # local passes the gate (fails later on missing base_url — proving the
    # offline gate itself did not block it)
    with pytest.raises(ConfigError, match="base_url"):
        build_provider("local", ProviderConfig(type="local"))


def test_offline_cli_flag_flows_into_config(tmp_path, monkeypatch):
    monkeypatch.delenv("FORGEX_OFFLINE", raising=False)
    monkeypatch.chdir(tmp_path)
    runner.invoke(app, ["init"])
    try:
        result = runner.invoke(app, ["--offline", "config", "show", "--json"])
        assert result.exit_code == 0, result.output
        assert json.loads(result.output)["research"]["enabled"] is False
    finally:
        os.environ.pop("FORGEX_OFFLINE", None)
tests/unit/test_run_cli.py ✚

python

import sys
from pathlib import Path

from typer.testing import CliRunner

from forgex.cli.app import app
from forgex.cli.commands.run import detect_run_command
from forgex.orchestration.stores import open_stores
from forgex.schemas.result import ModelUsageRecord

runner = CliRunner()


def test_detect_run_command(tmp_path):
    (tmp_path / "package.json").write_text(
        '{"scripts": {"start": "node server.js"}}', encoding="utf-8")
    assert detect_run_command(tmp_path) == ["npm", "start"]
    bare = tmp_path / "bare"
    bare.mkdir()
    assert detect_run_command(bare) is None


def test_cli_run_explicit_command(tmp_path: Path, monkeypatch):
    monkeypatch.chdir(tmp_path)
    runner.invoke(app, ["init"])
    result = runner.invoke(
        app, ["run", "--", sys.executable, "-c", "print('app running')"])
    assert result.exit_code == 0, result.output
    assert "app running" in result.output

    runner.invoke(app, ["init"])
    result = runner.invoke(app, ["run"])
    assert result.exit_code == 4  # ProjectError with usage hint


def test_status_shows_model_usage(tmp_path: Path, monkeypatch):
    monkeypatch.chdir(tmp_path)
    runner.invoke(app, ["init"])
    with open_stores(tmp_path) as stores:

            agent="architect", provider="groq", model="llama-3.3-70b-versatile",
            input_tokens=100, output_tokens=50, total_tokens=150))

2. The live proof (§76):

bash

cd examples/demo-project

When both pass: set the date in CHANGELOG.md, replace the repo URLs, git tag v0.1.0, push — CI builds the wheel, trusted publishing ships it, and pipx install forgex becomes real.
